- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Put above the tests in `crates/acd-voice/src/listen.rs`:

```rust
//! Triggers and recording rules (voice spec §4.1, §6.2). Plain Rust with
//! the time passed in, so every rule is tested on Linux. The platform sits
//! behind `SpeechInput`; on macOS the app implements it over the Swift
//! bridge, where the audio stays (step-2 plan S2).
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use serde::Serialize;

use crate::text::VoiceText;

/// Spec §6.2: a lost key-up must not leave the microphone on.
pub const MAX_RECORDING: Duration = Duration::from_secs(60);
/// Spec §6.2: shorter utterances are ignored.
pub const MIN_UTTERANCE: Duration = Duration::from_millis(300);
/// Less voiced audio than this is silent (plan S10).
pub const MIN_VOICED: Duration = Duration::from_millis(150);
/// After a stop the final text must arrive within this, or the recording
/// fails closed (plan S5).
pub const FINAL_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(5);

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Limits {
    pub max: Duration,
    pub final_timeout: Duration,
}

impl Default for Limits {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self { max: MAX_RECORDING, final_timeout: FINAL_TIMEOUT }
    }
}

/// What a trigger asks for (spec §4.1).
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum TriggerEvent {
    Start,
    Stop,
    Cancel,
}

/// The hold key (spec §6.2, plan S11). Down starts, up stops. Another key
/// while it is down is a chord (Option+e types "é"): that cancels, and the
/// following key-up does nothing.
#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct Hold {
    down: bool,
    chorded: bool,
}

impl Hold {
    pub fn key(&mut self, down: bool) -> Option<TriggerEvent> {
        match (self.down, down) {
            (false, true) => {
                self.down = true;
                self.chorded = false;
                Some(TriggerEvent::Start)
            }
            (true, false) => {
                self.down = false;
                (!std::mem::take(&mut self.chorded)).then_some(TriggerEvent::Stop)
            }
            // Auto-repeat, or a release we never saw pressed.
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    pub fn other_key(&mut self) -> Option<TriggerEvent> {
        if self.down && !self.chorded {
            self.chorded = true;
            return Some(TriggerEvent::Cancel);
        }
        None
    }

    /// The tap was disabled or restarted, so a key-up may be lost.
    pub fn reset(&mut self) -> Option<TriggerEvent> {
        let held = self.down && !self.chorded;
        *self = Self::default();
        held.then_some(TriggerEvent::Cancel)
    }
}

/// The toggle shortcut: the first press starts, the second stops.
pub fn toggle(listening: bool) -> TriggerEvent {
    if listening { TriggerEvent::Stop } else { TriggerEvent::Start }
}

/// Why a recording could not start or went wrong. Classified only: no
/// platform error text crosses into Rust (spec §6.3).
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum ListenError {
    MicrophoneDenied,
    MicrophoneUnavailable,
    SpeechDenied,
    SpeechUnavailable,
    AssetsMissing,
    Failed,
}

/// Why voice cannot listen on this machine (spec §9).
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum Unavailable {
    /// The backend has not answered yet.
    Checking,
    NotMac,
    OsTooOld,
    /// Built without the macOS 26 SDK (plan S16).
    SdkTooOld,
    TranscriberUnsupported,
    LocaleUnsupported,
    AssetsMissing,
}

/// What a backend reports (spec §4.1).
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct Capabilities {
    /// `None` when it can listen.
    pub unavailable: Option<Unavailable>,
    /// Without partials the HUD shows "Listening…" with no live text.
    pub streams_partials: bool,
    /// The analyzer's audio format, for information.
    pub sample_rate: u32,
}

impl Capabilities {
    pub const CHECKING: Self = Self { unavailable: Some(Unavailable::Checking), streams_partials: false, sample_rate: 0 };
}

/// From the platform. `generation` is the id `SpeechInput::start` got.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum ListenEvent {
    Partial { generation: u64, text: VoiceText },
    Final { generation: u64, text: VoiceText, audio: Duration, voiced: Duration },
    Error { generation: u64, error: ListenError },
}

impl ListenEvent {
    pub fn generation(&self) -> u64 {
        match self {
            ListenEvent::Partial { generation, .. }
            | ListenEvent::Final { generation, .. }
            | ListenEvent::Error { generation, .. } => *generation,
        }
    }
}

/// Capture and transcription (spec §4.1, one seam: plan S2). Every method
/// returns at once and never calls back synchronously; events arrive
/// later through `Controller::speech_event`.
pub trait SpeechInput: Send + Sync {
    fn capabilities(&self) -> Capabilities;
    /// Starts recording `generation`, stopping on its own after `max`
    /// (plus a margin). A live session of another generation is cancelled.
    fn start(&self, generation: u64, max: Duration) -> Result<(), ListenError>;
    /// Stops capture and finishes the transcript: a `Final` or an `Error`
    /// for `generation` follows.
    fn stop(&self, generation: u64);
    /// Stops capture and drops the session; nothing follows.
    fn cancel(&self, generation: u64);
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Ignored {
    TooShort,
    Silent,
    Empty,
}

/// How a recording ended.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Heard {
    Transcript(VoiceText),
    Ignored(Ignored),
    Failed(ListenError),
    /// The 60 s cap: discarded (plan S9).
    TooLong,
    /// No final within `FINAL_TIMEOUT` after the stop.
    TimedOut,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Progress {
    Partial(VoiceText),
    Done(Heard),
}

/// What a release did.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Stopped {
    /// Capture stopped; the final text follows.
    Finishing(u64),
    /// The release came at or after the cap: discard it and cancel (S9).
    TooLong(u64),
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Start {
    Started,
    /// A recording that was still finishing was dropped; cancel it.
    Replaced(u64),
    /// Already listening; the press changes nothing.
    Busy,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum Phase {
    #[default]
    Idle,
    Listening { generation: u64, since: Instant },
    Finishing { generation: u64, since: Instant },
}

/// One recording at a time (spec §6.2). Events of any other generation
/// are dropped (spec §4.2).
#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct Recorder {
    phase: Phase,
    limits: Limits,
}

impl Recorder {
    pub fn new(limits: Limits) -> Self {
        Self { phase: Phase::Idle, limits }
    }

    pub fn limits(&self) -> Limits {
        self.limits
    }

    /// The generation whose microphone is live.
    pub fn listening(&self) -> Option<u64> {
        match self.phase {
            Phase::Listening { generation, .. } => Some(generation),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    /// Listening, or stopped and waiting for the final text.
    pub fn active(&self) -> Option<u64> {
        match self.phase {
            Phase::Listening { generation, .. } | Phase::Finishing { generation, .. } => Some(generation),
            Phase::Idle => None,
        }
    }

    pub fn start(&mut self, generation: u64, now: Instant) -> Start {
        let result = match self.phase {
            Phase::Listening { .. } => return Start::Busy,
            Phase::Finishing { generation: old, .. } => Start::Replaced(old),
            Phase::Idle => Start::Started,
        };
        self.phase = Phase::Listening { generation, since: now };
        result
    }

    /// The key went up or the toggle was pressed again. At or after the cap
    /// the recording is too long, whether or not the timer ran yet (S9).
    pub fn stop(&mut self, now: Instant) -> Option<Stopped> {
        let Phase::Listening { generation, since } = self.phase else {
            return None;
        };
        if now.duration_since(since) >= self.limits.max {
            self.phase = Phase::Idle;
            return Some(Stopped::TooLong(generation));
        }
        self.phase = Phase::Finishing { generation, since: now };
        Some(Stopped::Finishing(generation))
    }

    /// Drops the recording; nothing it produces is routed.
    pub fn cancel(&mut self) -> Option<u64> {
        let generation = self.active()?;
        self.phase = Phase::Idle;
        Some(generation)
    }

    /// The 60 s cap and the final timeout. The caller cancels the returned
    /// generation on the platform.
    pub fn tick(&mut self, now: Instant) -> Option<(u64, Heard)> {
        let (generation, heard) = match self.phase {
            Phase::Listening { generation, since } if now.duration_since(since) >= self.limits.max => (generation, Heard::TooLong),
            Phase::Finishing { generation, since } if now.duration_since(since) >= self.limits.final_timeout => {
                (generation, Heard::TimedOut)
            }
            _ => return None,
        };
        self.phase = Phase::Idle;
        Some((generation, heard))
    }

    pub fn event(&mut self, ev: ListenEvent, now: Instant) -> Option<Progress> {
        if self.active() != Some(ev.generation()) {
            return None;
        }
        // The cap holds whatever order the timer and the events run in (S9).
        if let Phase::Listening { since, .. } = self.phase
            && now.duration_since(since) >= self.limits.max
        {
            self.phase = Phase::Idle;
            return Some(Progress::Done(Heard::TooLong));
        }
        match ev {
            ListenEvent::Partial { text, .. } => self.listening().map(|_| Progress::Partial(text)),
            ListenEvent::Final { text, audio, voiced, .. } => {
                self.phase = Phase::Idle;
                let heard = if audio >= self.limits.max { Heard::TooLong } else { judge(text, audio, voiced) };
                Some(Progress::Done(heard))
            }
            ListenEvent::Error { error, .. } => {
                self.phase = Phase::Idle;
                Some(Progress::Done(Heard::Failed(error)))
            }
        }
    }
}

/// Spec §6.2: under 0.3 s, silent (plan S10) or empty is ignored.
pub fn judge(text: VoiceText, audio: Duration, voiced: Duration) -> Heard {
    if audio < MIN_UTTERANCE {
        Heard::Ignored(Ignored::TooShort)
    } else if voiced < MIN_VOICED {
        Heard::Ignored(Ignored::Silent)
    } else if text.expose().trim().is_empty() {
        Heard::Ignored(Ignored::Empty)
    } else {
        Heard::Transcript(text)
    }
}

/// A scripted `SpeechInput` for tests: records calls, can refuse to start.
#[cfg(any(test, feature = "testing"))]
pub mod fake {
    use std::sync::Mutex;
    use std::time::Duration;

    use super::{Capabilities, ListenError, SpeechInput};

    #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
    pub enum Call {
        Start(u64),
        Stop(u64),
        Cancel(u64),
    }

    #[derive(Default)]
    pub struct FakeSpeechInput {
        pub calls: Mutex<Vec<Call>>,
        pub refuse: Mutex<Option<ListenError>>,
        /// `None` means available, with partials.
        pub caps: Mutex<Option<Capabilities>>,
    }

    impl FakeSpeechInput {
        pub fn calls(&self) -> Vec<Call> {
            self.calls.lock().unwrap().clone()
        }

        pub fn last_start(&self) -> Option<u64> {
            self.calls().iter().rev().find_map(|c| match c {
                Call::Start(g) => Some(*g),
                _ => None,
            })
        }
    }

    impl SpeechInput for FakeSpeechInput {
        fn capabilities(&self) -> Capabilities {
            self.caps.lock().unwrap().unwrap_or(Capabilities { unavailable: None, streams_partials: true, sample_rate: 16_000 })
        }
        fn start(&self, generation: u64, _max: Duration) -> Result<(), ListenError> {
            if let Some(e) = *self.refuse.lock().unwrap() {
                return Err(e);
            }
            self.calls.lock().unwrap().push(Call::Start(generation));
            Ok(())
        }
        fn stop(&self, generation: u64) {
            self.calls.lock().unwrap().push(Call::Stop(generation));
        }
        fn cancel(&self, generation: u64) {
            self.calls.lock().unwrap().push(Call::Cancel(generation));
        }
    }
}
```

```rust
/// Review Focus 3, spec §4.4: the press cancels the countdown before any
/// recognition, and the "cancel" said during that recording reports it.
#[tokio::test]
async fn the_trigger_cancels_a_countdown_at_press_and_a_spoken_cancel_reports_it() {
    let a = pending("m", "a");
    let s = select(snapshot(vec![a.clone()]), "m/a");
    let port = FakePort::with(&[&a]);
    let mut exec = Executor::new();
    say_shown(&mut exec, &port, &s, "approve").await;
    let (_, over) = exec.listen(&port);
    assert_eq!(port.cancelled(), ["act-0"], "cancelled at the press");
    assert_eq!(over, None);
    let r = say(&mut exec, &port, &s, "cancel").await;
    assert!(matches!(r.hud, Hud::Done { outcome: OutcomeView::Cancelled, .. }), "{:?}", r.hud);
    assert!(port.enqueued().len() == 1, "only the cancelled approve was ever enqueued");
}

#[test]
fn listening_is_part_of_the_frontend_contract() {
    let over = Hud::Error { refusal: Refusal::NoTarget, transcript: None };
    let hud = Hud::Listening { id: 3, partial: Some(VoiceText::new("app")), over: Some(Box::new(over)) };
    let v = serde_json::to_value(HudEvent::new(9, &hud)).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(v["id"], 3, "the HUD's Cancel names the recording");
    assert_eq!(v["hud"]["state"], "listening");
    assert_eq!(v["hud"]["partial"], "app");
    assert_eq!(v["hud"]["over"]["state"], "error");
    let bare = serde_json::to_value(Hud::Listening { id: 1, partial: None, over: None }).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(bare, serde_json::json!({"state": "listening", "id": 1, "partial": null, "over": null}));
    assert!(!hud.wants_key() && !hud.fades() && hud.awaiting_display().is_none());
    assert!(!format!("{hud:?}").contains("app\""), "Debug shows lengths only");
    for (r, s) in [
        (Refusal::MicrophoneDenied, "microphone_denied"),
        (Refusal::MicrophoneUnavailable, "microphone_unavailable"),
        (Refusal::SpeechDenied, "speech_denied"),
        (Refusal::SpeechUnavailable, "speech_unavailable"),
        (Refusal::SpeechAssetsMissing, "speech_assets_missing"),
        (Refusal::RecordingTooLong, "recording_too_long"),
        (Refusal::HoldKeyOff, "hold_key_off"),
    ] {
        assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(r).unwrap(), s);
    }
}

#[test]
fn listen_errors_map_to_classified_refusals() {
    use acd_voice::listen::{ListenError, Unavailable};
    assert_eq!(Refusal::from(ListenError::MicrophoneDenied), Refusal::MicrophoneDenied);
    assert_eq!(Refusal::from(ListenError::MicrophoneUnavailable), Refusal::MicrophoneUnavailable);
    assert_eq!(Refusal::from(ListenError::SpeechDenied), Refusal::SpeechDenied);
    assert_eq!(Refusal::from(ListenError::AssetsMissing), Refusal::SpeechAssetsMissing);
    assert_eq!(Refusal::from(ListenError::SpeechUnavailable), Refusal::SpeechUnavailable);
    assert_eq!(Refusal::from(ListenError::Failed), Refusal::SpeechUnavailable);
    assert_eq!(Refusal::from(Unavailable::AssetsMissing), Refusal::SpeechAssetsMissing);
    assert_eq!(Refusal::from(Unavailable::OsTooOld), Refusal::SpeechUnavailable);
}
```

```rust
impl From<crate::listen::ListenError> for Refusal {
    fn from(e: crate::listen::ListenError) -> Self {
        use crate::listen::ListenError as E;
        match e {
            E::MicrophoneDenied => Refusal::MicrophoneDenied,
            E::MicrophoneUnavailable => Refusal::MicrophoneUnavailable,
            E::SpeechDenied => Refusal::SpeechDenied,
            E::AssetsMissing => Refusal::SpeechAssetsMissing,
            E::SpeechUnavailable | E::Failed => Refusal::SpeechUnavailable,
        }
    }
}

impl From<crate::listen::Unavailable> for Refusal {
    fn from(u: crate::listen::Unavailable) -> Self {
        match u {
            crate::listen::Unavailable::AssetsMissing => Refusal::SpeechAssetsMissing,
            _ => Refusal::SpeechUnavailable,
        }
    }
}
```

```rust
    /// The trigger was pressed (spec §4.4, step-2 plan S7). A voice
    /// countdown, or a countdown step not yet shown, is cancelled at once.
    /// A step still thinking is dropped. A step that waits for input
    /// (confirm, which-one) stays and is returned, to show under the
    /// Listening state. A fired step keeps waiting for its outcome.
    ///
    /// Returns the recording's id, from the counter every HUD input id
    /// comes from, so the HUD's Cancel can name it.
    pub fn listen(&mut self, port: &dyn InboxPort) -> (u64, Option<Hud>) {
        match std::mem::take(&mut self.state) {
            State::Counting { action_id, .. } if port.cancel(&action_id) => self.trigger_cancelled = true,
            // Not enqueued yet (D22): its acknowledgement now matches nothing.
            State::Presenting { .. } => self.trigger_cancelled = true,
            // Its route result now belongs to an old utterance.
            State::Thinking(_) => self.utterance += 1,
            other => self.state = other,
        }
        let over = match &self.state {
            State::Confirm { .. } | State::WhichOne(..) => Some(self.waiting().hud),
            _ => None,
        };
        (self.next_proposal(), over)
    }

    /// The recording ended with nothing to route: too short, silent,
    /// empty, or cancelled (plan S8). Shows what the press cancelled, or
    /// the step that waits, or a fired step's progress, or nothing.
    pub fn resume(&mut self) -> Reaction {
        if std::mem::take(&mut self.trigger_cancelled) {
            return self.done(None, OutcomeView::Cancelled);
        }
        match &self.state {
            State::Counting { proposal, .. } | State::Sending { proposal, .. } => {
                show(Hud::Sending { proposal: self.view(proposal), too_late_to_cancel: false })
            }
            _ => self.waiting(),
        }
    }

    /// Nothing waits, counts down, sends or thinks, so a notice may take
    /// the HUD without hiding a step (Task 4 `Controller::notice`).
    pub fn is_idle(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(self.state, State::Idle | State::Shown)
    }

    /// The recording failed: microphone, speech, the 60 s cap or the final
    /// timeout (plan S8). A step that waits ends too, so nothing can be
    /// confirmed off screen. A fired step keeps waiting for its outcome.
    pub fn listen_failed(&mut self, refusal: Refusal) -> Reaction {
        self.trigger_cancelled = false;
        if matches!(self.state, State::Counting { .. } | State::Sending { .. }) {
            return show(Hud::Error { refusal, transcript: None });
        }
        self.error(refusal)
    }
```

```rust
use acd_voice::listen::ListenEvent;
use acd_voice::listen::fake::FakeSpeechInput;
use acd_voice::router::model::IntentModel;
use acd_voice::text::VoiceText;

/// `rig` with a scripted microphone and transcriber attached.
pub async fn speech_rig(agents: Vec<Value>) -> (Rig, Arc<FakeSpeechInput>) {
    speech_rig_with(agents, None, Display::Acks).await
}

pub async fn speech_rig_with(
    agents: Vec<Value>,
    model: Option<Arc<dyn IntentModel>>,
    display: Display,
) -> (Rig, Arc<FakeSpeechInput>) {
    let r = rig(agents, model, display).await;
    let speech = Arc::new(FakeSpeechInput::default());
    r.voice.attach_speech(speech.clone());
    (r, speech)
}

/// Waits until the HUD acknowledged proposal `id` (no fixed sleep).
pub async fn acked_until(sink: &Sink, id: u64) {
    tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(5), async {
        while !sink.acked.lock().unwrap().contains(&id) {
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(5)).await;
        }
    })
    .await
    .expect("the HUD acknowledged the proposal");
}

/// A final transcript of 900 ms with 600 ms of voice.
pub fn final_(generation: u64, text: &str) -> ListenEvent {
    ListenEvent::Final {
        generation,
        text: VoiceText::new(text),
        audio: Duration::from_millis(900),
        voiced: Duration::from_millis(600),
    }
}
```

Create `crates/acd-voice-client/tests/speech.rs`:

```rust
mod common;

use std::sync::Arc;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};
use std::time::Duration;

use acd_voice::hud::{Hud, OutcomeView, ProposalView};
use acd_voice::listen::fake::Call;
use acd_voice::listen::{Capabilities, Limits, ListenError, ListenEvent, Unavailable};
use acd_voice::refusal::Refusal;
use acd_voice::router::model::{IntentModel, ModelError};
use acd_voice::router::prompt::Prompt;
use acd_voice::snapshot::Presentation;
use acd_voice::text::VoiceText;
use acd_voice_client::Controller;
use common::*;
use serde_json::json;

fn ms(n: u64) -> Duration {
    Duration::from_millis(n)
}

fn countdown_started(h: &Hud) -> bool {
    matches!(h, Hud::Countdown { action_id: Some(_), .. })
}

fn first_countdown(s: &Sink) -> ProposalView {
    s.huds
        .lock()
        .unwrap()
        .iter()
        .find_map(|(_, h)| match h {
            Hud::Countdown { proposal, .. } => Some(proposal.clone()),
            _ => None,
        })
        .expect("a countdown")
}

async fn until(what: &str, pred: impl Fn() -> bool) {
    tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(5), async {
        while !pred() {
            tokio::time::sleep(ms(5)).await;
        }
    })
    .await
    .unwrap_or_else(|_| panic!("timed out waiting for {what}"));
}

/// Runs `f` once, at the next admission, with no lock held: the barrier
/// between "the transcript left the recorder" and "admission" (S23).
fn at_next_admission(voice: &Arc<Controller>, f: impl Fn(&Controller) + Send + Sync + 'static) -> Arc<AtomicBool> {
    let fired = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));
    let (flag, weak) = (fired.clone(), Arc::downgrade(voice));
    voice.on_admission_for_test(move || {
        if !flag.swap(true, Ordering::SeqCst)
            && let Some(v) = weak.upgrade()
        {
            f(&v);
        }
    });
    fired
}

async fn confirm_on_screen(r: &Rig) -> u64 {
    r.voice.publish(shown(Some("m1/a1"), &["m1/a1"]));
    r.voice.text_input("yes always".into(), true).await.unwrap();
    let Hud::Confirm { proposal_id, .. } = hud_until(&r.sink, "confirm", |h| matches!(h, Hud::Confirm { .. })).await else {
        unreachable!()
    };
    acked_until(&r.sink, proposal_id).await;
    proposal_id
}

/// A model that waits until released, and notes when its call is dropped.
struct Gated {
    gate: Arc<tokio::sync::Notify>,
    dropped: Arc<AtomicBool>,
}

#[async_trait::async_trait]
impl IntentModel for Gated {
    async fn plan(&self, _prompt: &Prompt) -> Result<VoiceText, ModelError> {
        struct OnDrop(Arc<AtomicBool>);
        impl Drop for OnDrop {
            fn drop(&mut self) {
                self.0.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);
            }
        }
        let armed = OnDrop(self.dropped.clone());
        self.gate.notified().await;
        std::mem::forget(armed);
        Ok(VoiceText::new(r#"{"step":"send","agent":"a1","text":"old"}"#))
    }
}

/// Plan S15: speech takes the typed path; the same proposal and frame.
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_spoken_approve_counts_down_like_a_typed_one() {
    let typed = rig(vec![pending_agent()], None, Display::Acks).await;
    typed.voice.publish(shown(Some("m1/a1"), &["m1/a1"]));
    typed.voice.text_input("approve".into(), true).await.unwrap();
    hud_until(&typed.sink, "typed countdown", countdown_started).await;

    let (r, speech) = speech_rig(vec![pending_agent()]).await;
    r.voice.publish(shown(Some("m1/a1"), &["m1/a1"]));
    r.voice.hold(true, true);
    let g = speech.last_start().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(r.sink.last(), Some(Hud::Listening { id: g, partial: None, over: None }));
    r.voice.hold(false, true);
    assert_eq!(speech.calls(), [Call::Start(g), Call::Stop(g)]);
    r.voice.speech_event(final_(g, "approve"));
    hud_until(&r.sink, "spoken countdown", countdown_started).await;

    assert_eq!(first_countdown(&typed.sink), first_countdown(&r.sink));
    let sent = frame(&r.f, "request.answer").await;
    assert_eq!((sent["request_id"].as_str(), sent["option_id"].as_str()), (Some("r1"), Some("allow")));
}

/// Plan S18: the indicator shows what Swift reports, never Rust's commands.
#[tokio::test]
async fn the_indicator_follows_swifts_capture_state() {
    let (r, speech) = speech_rig(vec![pending_agent()]).await;
    r.voice.hold(true, true);
    let g = speech.last_start().unwrap();
    assert!(r.sink.mics.lock().unwrap().is_empty(), "the press alone proves nothing");
    r.voice.capture(g, true);
    assert_eq!(*r.sink.mics.lock().unwrap(), [true]);
    r.voice.hold(false, true);
    assert_eq!(*r.sink.mics.lock().unwrap(), [true], "on until Swift says capture stopped");
    r.voice.capture(g, false);
    assert_eq!(*r.sink.mics.lock().unwrap(), [true, false]);
    r.voice.capture(g + 100, true);
    assert_eq!(speech.calls().last(), Some(&Call::Cancel(g + 100)), "capture nobody asked for is stopped");
    assert_eq!(*r.sink.mics.lock().unwrap(), [true, false]);
}

    assert!(frames(&r.f, "request.answer").is_empty(), "the old confirm answered nothing");

    r.voice.publish(shown(Some("m1/a1"), &["m1/a1"]));
    r.voice.hold(true, true);
    let g = speech.last_start().unwrap();
    r.voice.capture(g, true);
    r.voice.set_enabled(false);
    assert_eq!(speech.calls(), [Call::Start(g), Call::Cancel(g)]);
    r.voice.capture(g, false); // Swift reports the stop
    assert_eq!(*r.sink.mics.lock().unwrap(), [true, false]);
    r.voice.speech_event(final_(g, "approve"));
    tokio::time::sleep(ms(400)).await;
    assert!(frames(&r.f, "request.answer").is_empty());
}

/// Review Focus 5.
#[tokio::test]
async fn screen_lock_cancels_the_recording() {
    let (r, speech) = speech_rig(vec![pending_agent()]).await;
    r.voice.hold(true, true);
    let g = speech.last_start().unwrap();
    r.voice.system_stop();
    assert_eq!(speech.calls(), [Call::Start(g), Call::Cancel(g)]);
    assert_eq!(r.sink.last(), Some(Hud::Hidden));
    r.voice.hold(false, true);
    assert_eq!(speech.calls().len(), 2, "the key-up after a lock does nothing");
}

/// Review Focus 5, plan S24: nothing a lock leaves behind can be confirmed.
#[tokio::test]
async fn screen_lock_clears_a_waiting_confirm() {
    let (r, _speech) = speech_rig(vec![pending_agent()]).await;
    let proposal_id = confirm_on_screen(&r).await;
    r.voice.system_stop();
    assert_eq!(r.sink.last(), Some(Hud::Hidden));
    r.voice.confirm(proposal_id);
    r.voice.text_input("confirm".into(), true).await.unwrap();
    tokio::time::sleep(ms(400)).await;
    assert!(frames(&r.f, "request.answer").is_empty());
}

/// Review Focus 5, plan S24.
#[tokio::test]
async fn screen_lock_cancels_an_unsent_countdown() {
    let (r, _speech) = speech_rig(vec![pending_agent()]).await;
    r.voice.publish(shown(Some("m1/a1"), &["m1/a1"]));
    r.voice.text_input("approve".into(), true).await.unwrap();
    hud_until(&r.sink, "countdown", countdown_started).await;
    r.voice.system_stop();
    assert_eq!(r.sink.last(), Some(Hud::Hidden));
    tokio::time::sleep(ms(400)).await;
    assert!(frames(&r.f, "request.answer").is_empty());
}

/// Review Focus 5, plan S24: a model call in flight is cancelled.
#[tokio::test]
async fn screen_lock_drops_a_thinking_step() {
    let gate = Arc::new(tokio::sync::Notify::new());
    let dropped = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));
    let model: Arc<dyn IntentModel> = Arc::new(Gated { gate: gate.clone(), dropped: dropped.clone() });
    let (r, _speech) = speech_rig_with(vec![pending_agent(), working_agent()], Some(model), Display::Acks).await;
    r.voice.publish(shown(Some("m1/a1"), &["m1/a1", "m1/a2"]));
    let voice = r.voice.clone();
    let old = tokio::spawn(async move { voice.text_input("tell the box claude something".into(), true).await });
    hud_until(&r.sink, "thinking", |h| matches!(h, Hud::Thinking { .. })).await;
    r.voice.system_stop();
    assert_eq!(old.await.unwrap(), Ok(()));
    assert!(dropped.load(Ordering::SeqCst), "the model call was cancelled");
    gate.notify_waiters();
    assert_eq!(r.sink.last(), Some(Hud::Hidden));
    assert!(frames(&r.f, "agent.send").is_empty());
}

/// Plan S25.
#[tokio::test]
async fn esc_in_the_inbox_cancels_a_toggle_recording() {
    let (r, speech) = speech_rig(vec![pending_agent()]).await;
    assert!(!r.voice.escape_listening(), "nothing to cancel: the inbox handles Esc");
    r.voice.toggle(true);
    let g = speech.last_start().unwrap();
    assert!(r.voice.escape_listening());
    assert_eq!(speech.calls(), [Call::Start(g), Call::Cancel(g)]);
    assert!(!r.voice.listening());
    r.voice.speech_event(final_(g, "approve"));
    tokio::time::sleep(ms(400)).await;
    assert!(frames(&r.f, "request.answer").is_empty());
}

/// Plan S25: `cancel(None)` goes through the recorder too.
#[tokio::test]
async fn cancel_without_an_id_cancels_the_recording() {
    let (r, speech) = speech_rig(vec![pending_agent()]).await;
    r.voice.hold(true, true);
    let g = speech.last_start().unwrap();
    r.voice.cancel(None);
    assert_eq!(speech.calls(), [Call::Start(g), Call::Cancel(g)]);
    assert!(!r.voice.listening());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn the_toggle_starts_and_a_second_press_stops() {
    let (r, speech) = speech_rig(vec![pending_agent()]).await;
    r.voice.toggle(false);
    let g = speech.last_start().unwrap();
    assert!(r.voice.listening());
    r.voice.toggle(false);
    assert_eq!(speech.calls(), [Call::Start(g), Call::Stop(g)]);
    assert!(!r.voice.listening());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_press_with_voice_off_listens_to_nothing() {
    let (r, speech) = speech_rig(vec![pending_agent()]).await;
    r.voice.set_enabled(false);
    r.voice.hold(true, true);
    r.voice.toggle(true);
    assert!(speech.calls().is_empty());
    assert!(!r.sink.huds.lock().unwrap().iter().any(|(_, h)| matches!(h, Hud::Listening { .. })));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_refused_start_names_the_microphone_and_listens_to_nothing() {
    let (r, speech) = speech_rig(vec![pending_agent()]).await;
    *speech.refuse.lock().unwrap() = Some(ListenError::MicrophoneDenied);
    r.voice.hold(true, true);
    assert_eq!(r.sink.last(), Some(Hud::Error { refusal: Refusal::MicrophoneDenied, transcript: None }));
    assert!(!r.voice.listening());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn an_unavailable_backend_is_never_started() {
    let (r, speech) = speech_rig(vec![pending_agent()]).await;
    *speech.caps.lock().unwrap() =
        Some(Capabilities { unavailable: Some(Unavailable::AssetsMissing), streams_partials: false, sample_rate: 0 });
    r.voice.hold(true, true);
    assert_eq!(r.sink.last(), Some(Hud::Error { refusal: Refusal::SpeechAssetsMissing, transcript: None }));
    assert!(speech.calls().is_empty());
}

/// Spec §9 "HUD says so", without hiding a step that waits.
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_notice_never_hides_a_waiting_step() {
    let (r, _speech) = speech_rig(vec![pending_agent()]).await;
    r.voice.notice(Refusal::HoldKeyOff);
    assert_eq!(r.sink.last(), Some(Hud::Error { refusal: Refusal::HoldKeyOff, transcript: None }));
    confirm_on_screen(&r).await;
    r.voice.notice(Refusal::HoldKeyOff);
    assert!(matches!(r.sink.last(), Some(Hud::Confirm { .. })));
}

/// Plan S7: confirm stays on screen under Listening, and the spoken
/// "confirm" confirms it.
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_spoken_confirm_confirms_the_step_on_screen() {
    let (r, speech) = speech_rig(vec![pending_agent()]).await;
    confirm_on_screen(&r).await;
    r.voice.hold(true, true);
    assert!(matches!(r.sink.last(), Some(Hud::Listening { over: Some(_), .. })));
    let g = speech.last_start().unwrap();
    r.voice.hold(false, true);
    r.voice.speech_event(final_(g, "confirm"));
    let sent = frame(&r.f, "request.answer").await;
    assert_eq!(sent["option_id"].as_str(), Some("always"));
}

/// D22 for speech: a HUD that never acknowledges sends nothing.
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_withheld_display_acknowledgement_sends_nothing() {
    let (r, speech) = speech_rig_with(vec![pending_agent()], None, Display::Silent).await;
    r.voice.publish(shown(Some("m1/a1"), &["m1/a1"]));
    r.voice.hold(true, true);
    let g = speech.last_start().unwrap();
    r.voice.hold(false, true);
    r.voice.speech_event(final_(g, "approve"));
    hud_until(&r.sink, "display failed", |h| *h == Hud::Error { refusal: Refusal::DisplayFailed, transcript: None }).await;
    tokio::time::sleep(ms(400)).await;
    assert!(frames(&r.f, "request.answer").is_empty());
}

/// D22 for speech: a refused display fails closed at once.
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_refused_display_sends_nothing() {
    let (r, speech) = speech_rig(vec![pending_agent()]).await;
    r.sink.refuse_display.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);
    r.voice.publish(shown(Some("m1/a1"), &["m1/a1"]));
    r.voice.hold(true, true);
    let g = speech.last_start().unwrap();
    r.voice.hold(false, true);
    r.voice.speech_event(final_(g, "approve"));
    hud_until(&r.sink, "display failed", |h| *h == Hud::Error { refusal: Refusal::DisplayFailed, transcript: None }).await;
    tokio::time::sleep(ms(400)).await;
    assert!(frames(&r.f, "request.answer").is_empty());
}

/// The live revalidation still guards speech: a revision bump after the
/// press drops the step.
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_revision_bump_after_the_press_drops_the_spoken_step() {
    let (r, speech) = speech_rig(vec![pending_agent()]).await;
    r.voice.publish(shown(Some("m1/a1"), &["m1/a1"]));
    r.voice.hold(true, true);
    let g = speech.last_start().unwrap();
    let mut agent = pending_agent();
    agent["pending"]["revision"] = json!(2);
    r.c.send(json!({"type":"agent.updated","epoch":"e1","seq":2,"agent":agent})).await;
    view_until(&r.inbox, "revision 2", |v| {
        v.cards.iter().any(|c| c.agent.pending.as_ref().is_some_and(|p| p.revision == 2))
    })
    .await;
    r.voice.hold(false, true);
    r.voice.speech_event(final_(g, "approve"));
    hud_until(&r.sink, "dropped", |h| *h == Hud::Error { refusal: Refusal::SnapshotChanged, transcript: None }).await;
    tokio::time::sleep(ms(400)).await;
    assert!(frames(&r.f, "request.answer").is_empty());
}

/// A replaced confirmation: "confirm" answers only the one on screen, and
/// a click meant for the old one does nothing.
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_spoken_confirm_answers_only_the_current_confirmation() {
    let (r, speech) = speech_rig(vec![pending_agent()]).await;
    let old = confirm_on_screen(&r).await;
    r.voice.cancel(Some(old));
    hud_until(&r.sink, "cancelled", |h| matches!(h, Hud::Done { outcome: OutcomeView::Cancelled, .. })).await;
    let new = confirm_on_screen(&r).await;
    assert_ne!(old, new);
    r.voice.hold(true, true);
    let g = speech.last_start().unwrap();
    r.voice.hold(false, true);
    r.voice.speech_event(final_(g, "confirm"));
    frame(&r.f, "request.answer").await;
    r.voice.confirm(old);
    tokio::time::sleep(ms(400)).await;
    assert_eq!(frames(&r.f, "request.answer").len(), 1);
}
```

```rust
/// Spec §3.7 for speech: partial and final transcripts never reach the logs.
#[tokio::test(flavor = "current_thread")]
async fn spoken_text_never_reaches_the_logs() {
    use acd_voice::listen::ListenEvent;
    use acd_voice::text::VoiceText;

    let buffer = Buffer::default();
    let writer = buffer.clone();
    let subscriber = tracing_subscriber::fmt()
        .with_max_level(tracing::Level::TRACE)
        .with_ansi(false)
        .with_writer(move || writer.clone())
        .finish();
    let _guard = tracing::subscriber::set_default(subscriber);

    let (r, speech) = speech_rig(vec![pending_agent()]).await;
    r.voice.publish(shown(Some("m1/a1"), &["m1/a1"]));
    r.voice.hold(true, true);
    let g = speech.last_start().unwrap();
    r.voice.speech_event(ListenEvent::Partial { generation: g, text: VoiceText::new("SECRET-PARTIAL-a1") });
    r.voice.hold(false, true);
    r.voice.speech_event(final_(g, "approve SECRET-SPOKEN-55"));
    hud_until(&r.sink, "routed", |h| matches!(h, Hud::Error { .. })).await;

    let logs = String::from_utf8(buffer.0.lock().unwrap().clone()).unwrap();
    assert!(logs.contains("utterance routed") && logs.contains("source=\"speech\""), "{logs}");
    assert!(logs.contains("listening"), "{logs}");
    assert!(!logs.contains("SECRET"), "{logs}");
}
```

Replace `cancel` with:

```rust
    /// Esc or Cancel. `Some(id)` is the `input_id` of the state the HUD
    /// showed; a cancel for a state since replaced is ignored. `None` cancels
    /// whatever is current. A recording is cancelled through the recorder
    /// (plan S25), when `id` names it or is `None`.
    pub fn cancel(&self, id: Option<u64>) {
        let mut core = self.core.lock().unwrap();
        let active = core.recorder.active();
        if active.is_some() && (id.is_none() || id == active) {
            self.cancel_listening(&mut core);
            return;
        }
        if let Some(reaction) = core.exec.cancel(&self.port, id) {
            self.emit(&mut core.exec, reaction);
        }
    }
```

Add these methods inside `impl Controller`, after `cancel_thinking`:

```rust
    /// Attaches the platform's capture and transcription (spec §4.1).
    pub fn attach_speech(&self, input: Arc<dyn SpeechInput>) {
        *self.speech.lock().unwrap() = Some(input);
    }

    /// Test hook: shorter limits than 60 s and 5 s.
    #[doc(hidden)]
    pub fn set_listen_limits_for_test(&self, limits: Limits) {
        self.core.lock().unwrap().recorder = Recorder::new(limits);
    }

    /// Whether a recording is listening (not finishing).
    pub fn listening(&self) -> bool {
        self.core.lock().unwrap().recorder.listening().is_some()
    }

    /// The hold key went down or up. `panel_open` is read at the press,
    /// because the snapshot freezes then (spec §4.3, plan S6).
    pub fn hold(&self, down: bool, panel_open: bool) {
        let snapshot = down.then(|| self.freeze(panel_open));
        let mut core = self.core.lock().unwrap();
        if let Some(ev) = core.hold.key(down) {
            self.trigger(&mut core, ev, snapshot);
        }
    }

    /// Another key went down: a chord cancels (plan S11).
    pub fn other_key(&self) {
        let mut core = self.core.lock().unwrap();
        if let Some(ev) = core.hold.other_key() {
            self.trigger(&mut core, ev, None);
        }
    }

    /// The tap was disabled or is being replaced, so a key-up may be lost
    /// (plan S26).
    pub fn hold_lost(&self) {
        let mut core = self.core.lock().unwrap();
        if let Some(ev) = core.hold.reset() {
            self.trigger(&mut core, ev, None);
        }
    }

    /// The toggle shortcut: starts, or stops a recording.
    pub fn toggle(&self, panel_open: bool) {
        let snapshot = self.freeze(panel_open);
        let mut core = self.core.lock().unwrap();
        let ev = listen::toggle(core.recorder.listening().is_some());
        self.trigger(&mut core, ev, Some(snapshot));
    }

    /// Esc in the inbox panel (plan S25). True if it cancelled a recording;
    /// false lets the inbox handle Esc as before.
    pub fn escape_listening(&self) -> bool {
        let mut core = self.core.lock().unwrap();
        if core.recorder.active().is_none() {
            return false;
        }
        self.cancel_listening(&mut core);
        true
    }

    /// Swift reports the microphone started or stopped for `generation`
    /// (S18). Only this drives the indicator.
    pub fn capture(&self, generation: u64, on: bool) {
        let mut core = self.core.lock().unwrap();
        if on {
            if core.recorder.active() != Some(generation) {
                // Capture for a recording Rust no longer has: stop it.
                if let Some(input) = self.input() {
                    input.cancel(generation);
                }
                return;
            }
            if core.mic != Some(generation) {
                core.mic = Some(generation);
                self.sink.listening(true);
            }
        } else if core.mic == Some(generation) {
            core.mic = None;
            self.sink.listening(false);
        }
    }

    /// The bridge dropped an event (plan S5). It could have been a chord's
    /// other key, a key-up, a lock or a final, so the recording is no longer
    /// safe to route: cancel it, forget the hold state, and drop any
    /// transcript already on its way (S23).
    pub fn overflow(&self) {
        let mut core = self.core.lock().unwrap();
        tracing::info!(target: "acd_voice", "bridge overflow: recording invalidated");
        core.hold = Hold::default();
        core.epoch += 1;
        self.cancel_listening(&mut core);
    }

    /// The screen locked or the Mac is going to sleep (spec §6.2, plan S24).
    pub fn system_stop(&self) {
        let mut core = self.core.lock().unwrap();
        tracing::info!(target: "acd_voice", "voice invalidated for lock or sleep");
        core.hold = Hold::default();
        self.invalidate(&mut core);
    }

    /// A message that is not a step, such as "the hold key stopped working"
    /// (spec §9). Shown only when no step and no recording is on the HUD, so
    /// it never hides a step that waits.
    pub fn notice(&self, refusal: Refusal) {
        let mut core = self.core.lock().unwrap();
        if core.recorder.active().is_none() && core.exec.is_idle() {
            let hud = Hud::Error { refusal, transcript: None };
            self.emit(&mut core.exec, Reaction { hud, select: None });
        }
    }

    /// An event from the platform (spec §4.2). Events of any recording but
    /// the current one are dropped by the recorder.
    pub fn speech_event(&self, ev: ListenEvent) {
        let mut core = self.core.lock().unwrap();
        let Some(id) = core.recorder.active() else {
            return;
        };
        match core.recorder.event(ev, Instant::now()) {
            None => {}
            Some(Progress::Partial(text)) => {
                let hud = Hud::Listening { id, partial: Some(text), over: core.over.clone().map(Box::new) };
                self.emit(&mut core.exec, Reaction { hud, select: None });
            }
            Some(Progress::Done(heard)) => {
                // Too long or failed: make sure Swift lets go too.
                if !matches!(heard, Heard::Transcript(_) | Heard::Ignored(_))
                    && let Some(input) = self.input()
                {
                    input.cancel(id);
                }
                self.heard(&mut core, heard);
            }
        }
    }

    fn input(&self) -> Option<Arc<dyn SpeechInput>> {
        self.speech.lock().unwrap().clone()
    }

    /// Voice off, lock or sleep (plan S24): the recording is cancelled, an
    /// in-flight route is dropped and its model call cancelled (step-1 B4),
    /// an unsent voice countdown is cancelled, and every transcript,
    /// snapshot and shown confirmation is forgotten.
    fn invalidate(&self, core: &mut Core) {
        if let Some(generation) = core.recorder.cancel()
            && let Some(input) = self.input()
        {
            input.cancel(generation);
        }
        core.frozen = None;
        core.over = None;
        core.epoch += 1;
        core.session += 1;
        self.session_tx.send_replace(core.session);
        core.exec.reset(&self.port);
        self.emit(&mut core.exec, Reaction { hud: Hud::Hidden, select: None });
    }

    fn trigger(&self, core: &mut Core, ev: TriggerEvent, snapshot: Option<Snapshot>) {
        match ev {
            TriggerEvent::Start => {
                if let Some(snapshot) = snapshot {
                    self.start_listening(core, snapshot);
                }
            }
            TriggerEvent::Stop => self.stop_listening(core),
            TriggerEvent::Cancel => self.cancel_listening(core),
        }
    }

    fn start_listening(&self, core: &mut Core, snapshot: Snapshot) {
        if !core.enabled || core.recorder.listening().is_some() {
            return;
        }
        let Some(input) = self.input() else {
            return;
        };
        if let Some(why) = input.capabilities().unavailable {
            tracing::info!(target: "acd_voice", reason = ?why, "voice cannot listen");
            let r = core.exec.listen_failed(Refusal::from(why));
            self.emit(&mut core.exec, r);
            return;
        }
        let (id, over) = core.exec.listen(&self.port);
        core.epoch += 1;
        if let Start::Replaced(old) = core.recorder.start(id, Instant::now()) {
            input.cancel(old);
        }
        if let Err(e) = input.start(id, core.recorder.limits().max) {
            core.recorder.cancel();
            tracing::info!(target: "acd_voice", error = ?e, "recording refused");
            let r = core.exec.listen_failed(Refusal::from(e));
            self.emit(&mut core.exec, r);
            return;
        }
        core.frozen = Some(snapshot);
        core.over = over.clone();
        tracing::info!(target: "acd_voice", "listening");
        let hud = Hud::Listening { id, partial: None, over: over.map(Box::new) };
        self.emit(&mut core.exec, Reaction { hud, select: None });
        self.schedule_tick(core.recorder.limits().max);
    }

    fn stop_listening(&self, core: &mut Core) {
        match core.recorder.stop(Instant::now()) {
            None => {}
            Some(Stopped::Finishing(generation)) => {
                if let Some(input) = self.input() {
                    input.stop(generation);
                }
                self.schedule_tick(core.recorder.limits().final_timeout);
            }
            // Released at or after the cap, before the timer ran (S9).
            Some(Stopped::TooLong(generation)) => {
                if let Some(input) = self.input() {
                    input.cancel(generation);
                }
                self.heard(core, Heard::TooLong);
            }
        }
    }

    fn cancel_listening(&self, core: &mut Core) {
        let Some(generation) = core.recorder.cancel() else {
            return;
        };
        if let Some(input) = self.input() {
            input.cancel(generation);
        }
        core.frozen = None;
        core.over = None;
        tracing::info!(target: "acd_voice", "recording cancelled");
        let r = core.exec.resume();
        self.emit(&mut core.exec, r);
    }

    /// How the recording ended (plan S8, S9). A transcript is routed on its
    /// own task with the admission token taken here, under the lock (S23).
    fn heard(&self, core: &mut Core, heard: Heard) {
        let snapshot = core.frozen.take();
        core.over = None;
        let refusal = match heard {
            Heard::Transcript(text) => {
                let token = Admission { session: core.session, epoch: core.epoch };
                let (Some(snapshot), Some(me)) = (snapshot, self.me.upgrade()) else {
                    return;
                };
                self.runtime.spawn(async move {
                    let _ = me.utterance(text, snapshot, Source::Speech, Some(token)).await;
                });
                return;
            }
            Heard::Ignored(why) => {
                tracing::info!(target: "acd_voice", ignored = ?why, "recording ignored");
                let r = core.exec.resume();
                self.emit(&mut core.exec, r);
                return;
            }
            Heard::Failed(e) => Refusal::from(e),
            Heard::TooLong => Refusal::RecordingTooLong,
            Heard::TimedOut => Refusal::SpeechUnavailable,
        };
        tracing::info!(target: "acd_voice", refusal = ?refusal, "recording failed");
        let r = core.exec.listen_failed(refusal);
        self.emit(&mut core.exec, r);
    }

    fn schedule_tick(&self, after: Duration) {
        let me = self.me.clone();
        self.runtime.spawn(async move {
            tokio::time::sleep(after).await;
            if let Some(this) = me.upgrade() {
                this.tick();
            }
        });
    }

    /// The 60 s cap and the final timeout (spec §6.2, plan S5, S9).
    fn tick(&self) {
        let mut core = self.core.lock().unwrap();
        let Some((generation, heard)) = core.recorder.tick(Instant::now()) else {
            return;
        };
        if let Some(input) = self.input() {
            input.cancel(generation);
        }
        self.heard(&mut core, heard);
    }
```

Lock order: the core lock, then `speech` (inside `input()`). Nothing takes them the other way round. `self.runtime.spawn` under the core lock only queues the task.

```swift
import AVFoundation
import XCTest
@testable import VoiceBridge

final class AudioTests: XCTestCase {
    private func tone(amplitude: Float, frames: AVAudioFrameCount = 4800) -> AVAudioPCMBuffer {
        let format = AVAudioFormat(standardFormatWithSampleRate: 48_000, channels: 1)!
        let buffer = AVAudioPCMBuffer(pcmFormat: format, frameCapacity: frames)!
        buffer.frameLength = frames
        for i in 0..<Int(frames) {
            buffer.floatChannelData![0][i] = amplitude * sin(Float(i) * 2 * .pi * 440 / 48_000)
        }
        return buffer
    }

    /// Plan S10: voice is measured on the capture format, before conversion.
    func testAChunkIsMeasuredBeforeItIsConverted() throws {
        let target = try XCTUnwrap(AVAudioFormat(commonFormat: .pcmFormatInt16, sampleRate: 16_000, channels: 1, interleaved: true))
        let loud = tone(amplitude: 0.5)
        let converter = try XCTUnwrap(AVAudioConverter(from: loud.format, to: target))
        let chunk = try XCTUnwrap(Chunk.make(loud, converter: converter, to: target))
        XCTAssertTrue(chunk.voiced)
        XCTAssertEqual(chunk.seconds, 0.1, accuracy: 0.0001)
        XCTAssertEqual(chunk.buffer.format.sampleRate, 16_000)
        let quiet = try XCTUnwrap(Chunk.make(tone(amplitude: 0), converter: converter, to: target))
        XCTAssertFalse(quiet.voiced)
    }
}
```

```swift
import AVFoundation
import XCTest
@testable import VoiceBridge

/// Run by hand on a Mac (spec §10): `swift test --package-path app/src-tauri/swift --filter LiveTests`.
/// Skipped in `npm run verify`.
final class LiveTests: XCTestCase {
    #if compiler(>=6.2)
    func testTranscribesASpokenClip() async throws {
        guard #available(macOS 26.0, *) else { throw XCTSkip("needs macOS 26") }
        guard let locale = await Locales.resolve(), await Locales.isInstalled(locale) else {
            throw XCTSkip("no English speech model installed")
        }
        let url = FileManager.default.temporaryDirectory.appendingPathComponent("vb-live-\(UUID().uuidString).aiff")
        defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) }
        let say = Process()
        say.executableURL = URL(fileURLWithPath: "/usr/bin/say")
        say.arguments = ["-o", url.path, "approve the request"]
        try say.run()
        say.waitUntilExit()
        let file = try AVAudioFile(forReading: url)
        let transcription = Transcription(locale: locale)
        let format = try XCTUnwrap(await transcription.format())
        let converter = try XCTUnwrap(AVAudioConverter(from: file.processingFormat, to: format))
        let (chunks, sink) = AsyncStream.makeStream(of: Chunk.self)
        try await transcription.start(chunks) { _ in }
        while file.framePosition < file.length {
            let buffer = try XCTUnwrap(AVAudioPCMBuffer(pcmFormat: file.processingFormat, frameCapacity: 4096))
            try file.read(into: buffer)
            if let chunk = Chunk.make(buffer, converter: converter, to: format) { sink.yield(chunk) }
        }
        sink.finish()
        let heard = try await transcription.finish()
        XCTAssertTrue(heard.text.lowercased().contains("approve"))
        let seconds = Double(file.length) / file.processingFormat.sampleRate
        XCTAssertEqual(Double(heard.audioMs) / 1000, seconds, accuracy: 0.1)
        XCTAssertGreaterThan(heard.voicedMs, 300)
    }

    /// `cancel` ends a transcription that is waiting in `finish`.
    func testCancelEndsAWaitingFinish() async throws {
        guard #available(macOS 26.0, *) else { throw XCTSkip("needs macOS 26") }
        guard let locale = await Locales.resolve(), await Locales.isInstalled(locale) else {
            throw XCTSkip("no English speech model installed")
        }
        let transcription = Transcription(locale: locale)
        let (chunks, _sink) = AsyncStream.makeStream(of: Chunk.self) // never finishes
        try await transcription.start(chunks) { _ in }
        let finishing = Task { try? await transcription.finish() }
        await transcription.cancel()
        _ = await finishing.value // returns: nothing hangs
        _ = _sink
    }
    #endif

    func testCaptureStartsAndStops() throws {
        guard AVCaptureDevice.authorizationStatus(for: .audio) == .authorized else {
            throw XCTSkip("the microphone is not granted to the test runner")
        }
        let format = try XCTUnwrap(AVAudioFormat(commonFormat: .pcmFormatInt16, sampleRate: 16_000, channels: 1, interleaved: true))
        let capture = Capture()
        _ = try capture.start(to: format) {}
        capture.stop()
        capture.stop() // twice is safe
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Append to `Audio.swift`, and add `import VoiceBridgeC` at its top:

```swift
/// One capture buffer, measured in its own float format and converted to
/// the analyzer's format.
struct Chunk: @unchecked Sendable {
    let buffer: AVAudioPCMBuffer
    let seconds: Double
    let voiced: Bool

    static func make(_ buffer: AVAudioPCMBuffer, converter: AVAudioConverter, to format: AVAudioFormat) -> Chunk? {
        let voiced = Audio.levelDBFS(buffer) >= Audio.voicedDBFS
        guard let out = Audio.convert(buffer, with: converter, to: format) else { return nil }
        return Chunk(buffer: out, seconds: Double(buffer.frameLength) / buffer.format.sampleRate, voiced: voiced)
    }
}

/// Classified only: no framework error text crosses the bridge (spec §6.3).
enum BridgeError: Error {
    case micUnavailable
    case speechUnavailable

    var code: Int32 {
        switch self {
        case .micUnavailable: return VB_ERR_MIC_UNAVAILABLE
        case .speechUnavailable: return VB_ERR_SPEECH_UNAVAILABLE
        }
    }
}
```

`app/src-tauri/swift/Sources/VoiceBridge/Capture.swift`:

```swift
import AVFoundation

/// The microphone (spec §4.1, Capture).
protocol AudioCapture: AnyObject, Sendable {
    /// Starts the microphone. The stream ends when `stop` runs.
    func start(to format: AVAudioFormat, onLost: @escaping @Sendable () -> Void) throws -> AsyncStream<Chunk>
    /// Stops the microphone now and ends the stream. Synchronous, idempotent,
    /// any thread; returns after the engine stopped.
    func stop()
}

/// AVAudioEngine capture. The tap measures and converts one buffer and
/// yields it into a bounded stream. It takes no lock of ours and never calls
/// Rust (spec §4.2). Conversion in the tap follows Apple's SpeechAnalyzer
/// sample. `lock` guards `sink`, `observer` and `running`.
final class Capture: AudioCapture, @unchecked Sendable {
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private let engine = AVAudioEngine()
    private var sink: AsyncStream<Chunk>.Continuation?
    private var observer: NSObjectProtocol?
    private var running = false

    func start(to format: AVAudioFormat, onLost: @escaping @Sendable () -> Void) throws -> AsyncStream<Chunk> {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        let input = engine.inputNode
        let micFormat = input.outputFormat(forBus: 0)
        guard micFormat.sampleRate > 0, micFormat.channelCount > 0,
              let converter = AVAudioConverter(from: micFormat, to: format) else {
            throw BridgeError.micUnavailable
        }
        let (stream, sink) = AsyncStream.makeStream(of: Chunk.self, bufferingPolicy: .bufferingNewest(64))
        input.installTap(onBus: 0, bufferSize: 4096, format: micFormat) { buffer, _ in
            if let chunk = Chunk.make(buffer, converter: converter, to: format) { sink.yield(chunk) }
        }
        // The device went away or changed (spec §9: "Microphone unavailable").
        observer = NotificationCenter.default.addObserver(forName: .AVAudioEngineConfigurationChange, object: engine, queue: nil) { _ in
            onLost()
        }
        engine.prepare()
        do {
            try engine.start()
        } catch {
            input.removeTap(onBus: 0)
            sink.finish()
            if let observer { NotificationCenter.default.removeObserver(observer) }
            observer = nil
            throw BridgeError.micUnavailable
        }
        self.sink = sink
        running = true
        return stream
    }

    func stop() {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        guard running else { return }
        running = false
        if let observer { NotificationCenter.default.removeObserver(observer) }
        observer = nil
        engine.stop()
        engine.inputNode.removeTap(onBus: 0)
        sink?.finish()
        sink = nil
    }
}
```

`app/src-tauri/swift/Sources/VoiceBridge/Transcription.swift`:

```swift
import AVFoundation
import Foundation

/// How a recording sounded, measured in Swift; Rust applies the rules (S10).
struct Heard: Sendable, Equatable {
    let text: String
    let audioMs: UInt64
    let voicedMs: UInt64
}

/// Transcription (spec §4.1, Transcriber). A protocol so the session
/// lifecycle is tested with a fake (Task 5C).
protocol SpeechWork: AnyObject, Sendable {
    func format() async -> AVAudioFormat?
    /// Starts reading `chunks`; returns once the analyzer runs.
    func start(_ chunks: AsyncStream<Chunk>, partial: @escaping @Sendable (String) -> Void) async throws
    /// Waits for the chunks to end, then for the final text.
    func finish() async throws -> Heard
    /// Stops all work now, also while `finish` waits.
    func cancel() async
}

#if compiler(>=6.2)
import Speech

/// SpeechAnalyzer and SpeechTranscriber over a chunk stream. On-device only
/// (spec §3.6). Actor-isolated state; `cancel` can run while `finish` is
/// suspended, because actors are reentrant at each `await`.
@available(macOS 26.0, *)
actor Transcription: SpeechWork {
    private let transcriber: SpeechTranscriber
    private let analyzer: SpeechAnalyzer
    private var feed: AsyncStream<AnalyzerInput>.Continuation?
    private var reader: Task<String, Error>?
    private var pump: Task<Void, Never>?
    private var audio: Double = 0
    private var voiced: Double = 0

    init(locale: Locale) {
        transcriber = SpeechTranscriber(locale: locale, transcriptionOptions: [], reportingOptions: [.volatileResults], attributeOptions: [])
        analyzer = SpeechAnalyzer(modules: [transcriber])
    }

    func format() async -> AVAudioFormat? {
        await SpeechAnalyzer.bestAvailableAudioFormat(compatibleWith: [transcriber])
    }

    /// `partial` gets the live text at most every 100 ms, latest wins (S5).
    func start(_ chunks: AsyncStream<Chunk>, partial: @escaping @Sendable (String) -> Void) async throws {
        let (inputs, feed) = AsyncStream.makeStream(of: AnalyzerInput.self, bufferingPolicy: .bufferingNewest(64))
        self.feed = feed
        let transcriber = self.transcriber
        reader = Task {
            var final = ""
            var last = ContinuousClock.now - .seconds(1)
            for try await result in transcriber.results {
                if result.isFinal {
                    final += String(result.text.characters)
                } else if last.duration(to: .now) >= .milliseconds(100) {
                    last = .now
                    partial(final + String(result.text.characters))
                }
            }
            return final
        }
        try await analyzer.start(inputSequence: inputs)
        pump = Task { await self.consume(chunks) }
    }

    private func consume(_ chunks: AsyncStream<Chunk>) async {
        for await chunk in chunks {
            audio += chunk.seconds
            if chunk.voiced { voiced += chunk.seconds }
            feed?.yield(AnalyzerInput(buffer: chunk.buffer))
        }
        feed?.finish()
    }

    func finish() async throws -> Heard {
        await pump?.value
        try await analyzer.finalizeAndFinishThroughEndOfInput()
        let text = try await reader?.value ?? ""
        return Heard(text: text, audioMs: UInt64(audio * 1000), voicedMs: UInt64(voiced * 1000))
    }

    func cancel() async {
        pump?.cancel()
        feed?.finish()
        reader?.cancel()
        await analyzer.cancelAndFinishNow()
    }
}
#endif
```

Create `app/src-tauri/swift/Tests/VoiceBridgeTests/OutboxTests.swift`:

```swift
import XCTest
import VoiceBridgeC
@testable import VoiceBridge

/// Collects what the bridge delivers, from the event queue.
final class Box: @unchecked Sendable {
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var items: [Event] = []
    func append(_ e: Event) { lock.lock(); items.append(e); lock.unlock() }
    var all: [Event] { lock.lock(); defer { lock.unlock() }; return items }
}

final class OutboxTests: XCTestCase {
    private func bridge() -> (Bridge, Box) {
        let bridge = Bridge()
        let box = Box()
        bridge.install { box.append($0) }
        return (bridge, box)
    }

    /// Review Focus 4: late events from an old generation are dropped (spec §10).
    func testSessionEventsOfAnotherGenerationAreDropped() {
        let (bridge, box) = bridge()
        bridge.setCurrent(5)
        bridge.emitSession(Event(generation: 4, kind: VB_EV_FINAL, text: "approve"))
        bridge.emitPartial(5, "de")
        bridge.emitSession(Event(generation: 0, kind: VB_EV_FINAL, text: "zero is never a session"))
        bridge.events.sync {}
        bridge.setCurrent(0)
        bridge.emitSession(Event(generation: 5, kind: VB_EV_FINAL, text: "deny"))
        bridge.events.sync {}
        XCTAssertEqual(box.all.map(\.generation), [5])
        XCTAssertEqual(box.all.map(\.kind), [VB_EV_PARTIAL])
    }

    /// The generation is checked at delivery: an event queued before a cancel is dropped.
    func testAnEventQueuedBeforeACancelIsDropped() {
        let (bridge, box) = bridge()
        bridge.setCurrent(7)
        bridge.events.suspend()
        bridge.emitSession(Event(generation: 7, kind: VB_EV_FINAL, text: "approve"))
        bridge.setCurrent(0)
        bridge.events.resume()
        bridge.events.sync {}
        XCTAssertTrue(box.all.isEmpty)
    }

    /// Review Focus 2, plan S5: the backlog is bounded, and an overflow is
    /// reported before anything that follows it.
    func testOverflowIsLatchedAndDeliveredFirst() {
        let (bridge, box) = bridge()
        bridge.events.suspend()
        for _ in 0..<(Bridge.capacity + 10) {
            bridge.emit(Event(kind: VB_EV_OTHER_KEY))
        }
        bridge.events.resume()
        bridge.events.sync {}
        XCTAssertEqual(box.all.first?.kind, VB_EV_OVERFLOW)
        XCTAssertEqual(box.all.count, Bridge.capacity + 1)
        bridge.emit(Event(kind: VB_EV_OTHER_KEY))
        bridge.events.sync {}
        XCTAssertEqual(box.all.filter { $0.kind == VB_EV_OVERFLOW }.count, 1, "the latch clears once delivered")
    }

    /// Plan S5, S18: partials and capture state keep only the latest.
    func testPartialsAndCaptureStateCoalesce() {
        let (bridge, box) = bridge()
        bridge.setCurrent(3)
        bridge.events.suspend()
        for i in 0..<10 { bridge.emitPartial(3, "word \(i)") }
        bridge.emitCapture(3, on: true)
        bridge.emitCapture(3, on: false)
        bridge.events.resume()
        bridge.events.sync {}
        XCTAssertEqual(box.all.map(\.kind), [VB_EV_CAPTURE, VB_EV_PARTIAL])
        XCTAssertEqual(box.all.first?.value, 0, "the latest capture state")
        XCTAssertEqual(box.all.last?.text, "word 9")
    }

    /// Review Focus 5: a lock is latched, never dropped, and goes first.
    func testALockIsNeverDropped() {
        let (bridge, box) = bridge()
        bridge.events.suspend()
        for _ in 0..<(Bridge.capacity + 10) { bridge.emit(Event(kind: VB_EV_OTHER_KEY)) }
        bridge.emitSystem(VB_SYS_SCREEN_LOCKED)
        bridge.events.resume()
        bridge.events.sync {}
        XCTAssertEqual(box.all.prefix(2).map(\.kind), [VB_EV_OVERFLOW, VB_EV_SYSTEM])
    }

    /// Spec §4.2: one drain block at a time, however many events arrive.
    func testOneDrainBlockAtATime() {
        let (bridge, _) = bridge()
        bridge.events.suspend()
        for _ in 0..<1000 { bridge.emitPartial(1, "x") }
        XCTAssertEqual(bridge.scheduledForTest, true)
        bridge.events.resume()
        bridge.events.sync {}
        XCTAssertEqual(bridge.scheduledForTest, false)
    }
}
```

Replace `final class Bridge` in `Bridge.swift` (keep `struct Event` and `vb_init`) with:

```swift
/// The way out of Swift (plan S5). Bounded and coalescing:
/// - one pending capture state (latest wins) and one pending partial
///   (latest wins);
/// - one latched lock or sleep code;
/// - a FIFO of at most `capacity` other events. When it is full, the new
///   event is dropped and an overflow is latched, delivered first next time.
/// At most one drain block is queued on `events` at any time, and the Rust
/// callback runs only there: never on the audio thread, never twice at
/// once. `lock` guards every field below it.
final class Bridge: @unchecked Sendable {
    static let shared = Bridge()
    static let capacity = 64

    let events = DispatchQueue(label: "dev.agentcaller.voice.events")
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var sink: ((Event) -> Void)?
    private var current: UInt64 = 0
    private var fifo: [Event] = []
    private var partial: Event?
    private var capture: Event?
    private var system: UInt32?
    private var overflowed = false
    private var scheduled = false

    func install(_ sink: @escaping (Event) -> Void) {
        lock.lock()
        self.sink = sink
        lock.unlock()
    }

    var isInitialized: Bool {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        return sink != nil
    }

    /// The live session; its events are delivered, any other session's are
    /// dropped at delivery (spec §4.2). 0 means none.
    func setCurrent(_ generation: UInt64) {
        lock.lock()
        current = generation
        lock.unlock()
    }

    var currentGeneration: UInt64 {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        return current
    }

    var scheduledForTest: Bool {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        return scheduled
    }

    func emit(_ e: Event) {
        push {
            if fifo.count >= Bridge.capacity {
                overflowed = true
            } else {
                fifo.append(e)
            }
        }
    }

    /// A final or error of session `e.generation`; checked at delivery.
    func emitSession(_ e: Event) {
        emit(e)
    }

    func emitPartial(_ generation: UInt64, _ text: String) {
        push { partial = Event(generation: generation, kind: VB_EV_PARTIAL, text: text) }
    }

    /// Never filtered: the indicator must learn every stop (S18).
    func emitCapture(_ generation: UInt64, on: Bool) {
        push { capture = Event(generation: generation, kind: VB_EV_CAPTURE, value: on ? 1 : 0) }
    }

    func emitSystem(_ code: UInt32) {
        push { system = code }
    }

    static func isStale(_ e: Event, current: UInt64) -> Bool {
        switch e.kind {
        case VB_EV_PARTIAL, VB_EV_FINAL, VB_EV_ERROR: return e.generation == 0 || e.generation != current
        default: return false
        }
    }

    private func push(_ change: () -> Void) {
        lock.lock()
        change()
        let schedule = !scheduled
        scheduled = true
        lock.unlock()
        if schedule {
            events.async { self.drain() }
        }
    }

    /// Delivers what is pending: overflow, lock, capture state, the FIFO in
    /// order, the latest partial.
    private func drain() {
        while true {
            lock.lock()
            var batch: [Event] = []
            if overflowed {
                batch.append(Event(kind: VB_EV_OVERFLOW))
                overflowed = false
            }
            if let code = system {
                batch.append(Event(kind: VB_EV_SYSTEM, code: code))
                system = nil
            }
            if let c = capture {
                batch.append(c)
                capture = nil
            }
            batch += fifo
            fifo.removeAll(keepingCapacity: true)
            if let p = partial {
                batch.append(p)
                partial = nil
            }
            if batch.isEmpty {
                scheduled = false
                lock.unlock()
                return
            }
            let current = self.current
            let sink = self.sink
            lock.unlock()
            for e in batch where !Bridge.isStale(e, current: current) {
                sink?(e)
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```swift
import Foundation
import VoiceBridgeC
#if compiler(>=6.2)
import Speech
#endif

/// Capabilities and asset downloads (plan S4, S21), each on its own task,
/// never on the session path. `lock` guards the cached values.
final class Assets: @unchecked Sendable {
    static let shared = Assets()

    /// Whether SpeechTranscriber streams volatile results in the bundle.
    /// From the step-2 spike record (S22); true unless it showed none.
    static let partialsStream = true

    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var cached: Event?
    private var resolved: Locale?
    private var installing = false

    /// The last VB_EV_CAPS, for vb_session_start's synchronous checks.
    var caps: Event? {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        return cached
    }

    /// The locale to transcribe, set only when speech is available.
    var locale: Locale? {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        return resolved
    }

    func query() {
        Task.detached {
            let (event, locale) = await Assets.check()
            self.lock.lock()
            self.cached = event
            self.resolved = locale
            self.lock.unlock()
            Bridge.shared.emit(event)
        }
    }

    func install() {
        lock.lock()
        let busy = installing
        installing = true
        lock.unlock()
        if busy { return }
        Task.detached {
            let code = await Assets.download()
            self.lock.lock()
            self.installing = false
            self.lock.unlock()
            Bridge.shared.emit(Event(kind: VB_EV_ASSETS, code: UInt32(bitPattern: code)))
            self.query()
        }
    }

    private static func capsEvent(_ unavailable: UInt32, _ rate: UInt32 = 0, _ locale: Locale? = nil) -> Event {
        Event(kind: VB_EV_CAPS, code: unavailable, value: unavailable == VB_UA_NONE && partialsStream ? 1 : 0,
              value2: rate, text: locale?.identifier)
    }

    static func check() async -> (Event, Locale?) {
        #if compiler(>=6.2)
        guard #available(macOS 26.0, *) else { return (capsEvent(VB_UA_OS_TOO_OLD), nil) }
        guard SpeechTranscriber.isAvailable else { return (capsEvent(VB_UA_TRANSCRIBER_UNSUPPORTED), nil) }
        guard let locale = await Locales.resolve() else { return (capsEvent(VB_UA_LOCALE_UNSUPPORTED), nil) }
        guard await Locales.isInstalled(locale) else { return (capsEvent(VB_UA_ASSETS_MISSING, 0, locale), nil) }
        let format = await Transcription(locale: locale).format()
        return (capsEvent(VB_UA_NONE, UInt32(format?.sampleRate ?? 0), locale), locale)
        #else
        return (capsEvent(VB_UA_SDK_TOO_OLD), nil)
        #endif
    }

    /// The system download for the English model (spec §9, plan S21).
    static func download() async -> Int32 {
        #if compiler(>=6.2)
        guard #available(macOS 26.0, *), let locale = await Locales.resolve() else { return VB_ERR_SPEECH_UNAVAILABLE }
        let module = SpeechTranscriber(locale: locale, transcriptionOptions: [], reportingOptions: [], attributeOptions: [])
        do {
            if let request = try await AssetInventory.assetInstallationRequest(supporting: [module]) {
                try await request.downloadAndInstall()
            }
            return VB_OK
        } catch {
            return VB_ERR_FAILED
        }
        #else
        return VB_ERR_SPEECH_UNAVAILABLE
        #endif
    }
}

@_cdecl("vb_permission_status")
public func vb_permission_status(_ permission: UInt32) -> UInt32 {
    Permissions.status(permission)
}

@_cdecl("vb_permission_request")
public func vb_permission_request(_ permission: UInt32) {
    Permissions.request(permission)
}

@_cdecl("vb_query_capabilities")
public func vb_query_capabilities() {
    Assets.shared.query()
}

@_cdecl("vb_install_assets")
public func vb_install_assets() {
    Assets.shared.install()
}
```

```c
/* Starts recording `generation` (non-zero). Returns VB_OK, or at once:
 * VB_ERR_NOT_INITIALIZED, VB_ERR_FAILED (generation 0), VB_ERR_MIC_DENIED,
 * VB_ERR_SPEECH_DENIED (only when speech_required is non-zero, plan S14),
 * VB_ERR_SPEECH_UNAVAILABLE or VB_ERR_ASSETS_MISSING. Every other session
 * is cancelled first, and its capture has stopped before this returns (S4).
 * Capture stops on its own max_ms after it started (S9). Later: VB_EV_CAPTURE
 * when the microphone starts and stops, VB_EV_PARTIAL, then VB_EV_FINAL or
 * VB_EV_ERROR. */
int32_t vb_session_start(uint64_t generation, uint32_t max_ms, uint32_t speech_required);

/* Stops capture before returning, and lets the transcript finish:
 * VB_EV_FINAL or VB_EV_ERROR follows. The finishing session stays
 * cancellable. Ignored for an unknown generation. Never queued (S4). */
void vb_session_stop(uint64_t generation);

/* Stops capture before returning and cancels all of the session's work,
 * finishing or not. Nothing follows but VB_EV_CAPTURE off. Ignored for an
 * unknown generation. Never queued (S4). */
void vb_session_cancel(uint64_t generation);

/* Task 7A: the listen-only hold-key tap. */
int32_t vb_tap_start(uint16_t keycode, uint64_t down_mask);
void vb_tap_stop(void);
```
