```swift
import AVFoundation
import XCTest
import VoiceBridgeC
@testable import VoiceBridge

final class Gate: @unchecked Sendable {
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var open = false
    private var waiters: [CheckedContinuation<Void, Never>] = []

    func wait() async {
        await withCheckedContinuation { (c: CheckedContinuation<Void, Never>) in
            lock.lock()
            if open {
                lock.unlock()
                c.resume()
            } else {
                waiters.append(c)
                lock.unlock()
            }
        }
    }

    func release() {
        lock.lock()
        open = true
        let w = waiters
        waiters = []
        lock.unlock()
        w.forEach { $0.resume() }
    }
}

final class FakeCapture: AudioCapture, @unchecked Sendable {
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var sink: AsyncStream<Chunk>.Continuation?
    private var startCount = 0

    var running: Bool { lock.lock(); defer { lock.unlock() }; return sink != nil }
    var starts: Int { lock.lock(); defer { lock.unlock() }; return startCount }

    func start(to format: AVAudioFormat, onLost: @escaping @Sendable () -> Void) throws -> AsyncStream<Chunk> {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        startCount += 1
        let (stream, sink) = AsyncStream.makeStream(of: Chunk.self)
        self.sink = sink
        return stream
    }

    func stop() {
        lock.lock()
        let s = sink
        sink = nil
        lock.unlock()
        s?.finish()
    }
}

final class FakeWork: SpeechWork, @unchecked Sendable {
    let formatGate = Gate()
    let finishGate = Gate()
    var gateFormat = false
    var hangFinish = false
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var chunks: AsyncStream<Chunk>?
    private var cancelledFlag = false

    var cancelled: Bool { lock.lock(); defer { lock.unlock() }; return cancelledFlag }

    func format() async -> AVAudioFormat? {
        if gateFormat { await formatGate.wait() }
        return AVAudioFormat(standardFormatWithSampleRate: 16_000, channels: 1)
    }

    func start(_ chunks: AsyncStream<Chunk>, partial: @escaping @Sendable (String) -> Void) async throws {
        lock.lock()
        self.chunks = chunks
        lock.unlock()
    }

    func finish() async throws -> Heard {
        lock.lock()
        let c = chunks
        lock.unlock()
        if let c { for await _ in c {} }
        if hangFinish {
            await finishGate.wait()
            throw CancellationError()
        }
        return Heard(text: "approve", audioMs: 900, voicedMs: 600)
    }

    func cancel() async {
        lock.lock()
        cancelledFlag = true
        lock.unlock()
        finishGate.release()
    }
}

final class SessionTests: XCTestCase {
    private func until(_ what: String, _ cond: () -> Bool, file: StaticString = #filePath, line: UInt = #line) {
        let deadline = Date().addingTimeInterval(2)
        while !cond() {
            if Date() > deadline { return XCTFail("timed out: \(what)", file: file, line: line) }
            usleep(2_000)
        }
    }

    private final class Rig {
        let bridge = Bridge()
        let box = Box()
        let work = FakeWork()
        private let lock = NSLock()
        private var made: [FakeCapture] = []
        private var finished: Set<UInt64> = []
        lazy var sessions: Sessions = {
            let s = Sessions(bridge: bridge) { [unowned self] g in
                let capture = FakeCapture()
                self.lock.lock(); self.made.append(capture); self.lock.unlock()
                return Session(generation: g, capture: capture, work: self.work, bridge: self.bridge)
            }
            s.onDone = { [unowned self] g in self.lock.lock(); self.finished.insert(g); self.lock.unlock() }
            return s
        }()

        init() { bridge.install { [box] in box.append($0) } }

        func capture(_ i: Int) -> FakeCapture? { lock.lock(); defer { lock.unlock() }; return i < made.count ? made[i] : nil }
        func done(_ g: UInt64) -> Bool { lock.lock(); defer { lock.unlock() }; return finished.contains(g) }
        func kinds() -> [UInt32] { bridge.events.sync {}; return box.all.map(\.kind) }
    }

    /// Plan S4: stop ends capture before it returns; the finishing session
    /// stays tracked, and cancel still reaches it.
    func testStopEndsCaptureAndTheFinishingSessionStaysCancellable() {
        let r = Rig()
        r.work.hangFinish = true
        XCTAssertEqual(r.sessions.start(1, maxMs: 60_000), VB_OK)
        until("capture") { r.capture(0)?.running == true }
        r.sessions.stop(1)
        XCTAssertFalse(r.capture(0)!.running, "the microphone stopped before stop returned")
        XCTAssertEqual(r.sessions.count, 1, "the finishing session is still tracked")
        r.sessions.cancel(1)
        until("work cancelled") { r.work.cancelled }
        until("session done") { r.done(1) }
        XCTAssertEqual(r.sessions.count, 0)
        XCTAssertEqual(r.kinds().filter { $0 == VB_EV_CAPTURE }.count, 2, "on, then off")
    }

    /// Review Focus 5: a lock reaches a session that is still finishing.
    func testCancelAllReachesFinishingSessions() {
        let r = Rig()
        r.work.hangFinish = true
        _ = r.sessions.start(1, maxMs: 60_000)
        until("capture") { r.capture(0)?.running == true }
        r.sessions.stop(1)
        r.sessions.cancelAll()
        until("work cancelled") { r.work.cancelled }
        until("session done") { r.done(1) }
        XCTAssertFalse(r.kinds().contains(VB_EV_FINAL))
    }

    /// Plan S4: a new start stops the old capture before it returns.
    func testANewStartStopsTheOldCaptureFirst() {
        let r = Rig()
        _ = r.sessions.start(1, maxMs: 60_000)
        until("capture 1") { r.capture(0)?.running == true }
        _ = r.sessions.start(2, maxMs: 60_000)
        XCTAssertFalse(r.capture(0)!.running, "stopped inside start(2)")
        until("capture 2") { r.capture(1)?.running == true }
        XCTAssertEqual(r.bridge.currentGeneration, 2)
    }

    func testCancelBeforeCaptureNeverOpensTheMicrophone() {
        let r = Rig()
        r.work.gateFormat = true
        _ = r.sessions.start(1, maxMs: 60_000)
        r.sessions.cancel(1)
        r.work.formatGate.release()
        until("session done") { r.done(1) }
        XCTAssertEqual(r.capture(0)?.starts, 0)
        XCTAssertFalse(r.kinds().contains(VB_EV_CAPTURE))
    }

    /// A release before the microphone opened ends with nothing heard,
    /// which Rust ignores as too short.
    func testAReleaseBeforeCaptureEndsWithNothingHeard() {
        let r = Rig()
        r.work.gateFormat = true
        _ = r.sessions.start(1, maxMs: 60_000)
        r.sessions.stop(1)
        r.work.formatGate.release()
        until("session done") { r.done(1) }
        XCTAssertEqual(r.capture(0)?.starts, 0)
        _ = r.kinds() // flush the event queue
        let final = r.box.all.first { $0.kind == VB_EV_FINAL }
        XCTAssertEqual(final?.audioMs, 0)
    }

    /// Plan S9: the failsafe counts from capture start and stops capture.
    func testTheFailsafeStopsCaptureAtTheCap() {
        let r = Rig()
        _ = r.sessions.start(1, maxMs: 50)
        until("capture") { r.capture(0)?.starts == 1 }
        until("failsafe") { r.capture(0)?.running == false }
        until("session done") { r.done(1) }
    }

    func testAFinalCarriesItsMeasurements() {
        let r = Rig()
        _ = r.sessions.start(1, maxMs: 60_000)
        until("capture") { r.capture(0)?.running == true }
        r.sessions.stop(1)
        until("final") { r.box.all.contains { $0.kind == VB_EV_FINAL } }
        let final = r.box.all.first { $0.kind == VB_EV_FINAL }!
        XCTAssertEqual(final.generation, 1)
        XCTAssertEqual(final.text, "approve")
        XCTAssertEqual(final.audioMs, 900)
        XCTAssertEqual(final.voicedMs, 600)
    }

    func testStartRefusesGenerationZero() {
        XCTAssertEqual(Rig().sessions.start(0, maxMs: 60_000), VB_ERR_FAILED)
    }
}
```

`app/src-tauri/swift/Sources/VoiceBridge/Sessions.swift`:

```swift
import AVFoundation
import Foundation
import VoiceBridgeC

/// One recording (plan S4). `stopCapture` and `cancel` are synchronous and
/// take only this session's lock, so no queue sits between them and the
/// microphone. The work runs on `task`. `lock` guards every var below it.
final class Session: @unchecked Sendable {
    let generation: UInt64
    private let capture: AudioCapture
    private let work: SpeechWork
    private let bridge: Bridge
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var started = false
    private var captureEnded = false
    private var cancelled = false
    private var stoppedAt: ContinuousClock.Instant?
    private var task: Task<Void, Never>?
    private var failsafe: DispatchWorkItem?

    init(generation: UInt64, capture: AudioCapture, work: SpeechWork, bridge: Bridge) {
        self.generation = generation
        self.capture = capture
        self.work = work
        self.bridge = bridge
    }

    /// `done` runs once all of the session's work ended, cancelled or not.
    func run(maxMs: UInt32, done: @escaping @Sendable () -> Void) {
        let t = Task { [self] in
            await body(maxMs: maxMs)
            done()
        }
        lock.lock()
        task = t
        let late = cancelled
        lock.unlock()
        if late { t.cancel() }
    }

    private func body(maxMs: UInt32) async {
        guard let format = await work.format() else { return fail(VB_ERR_SPEECH_UNAVAILABLE) }
        let chunks: AsyncStream<Chunk>
        lock.lock()
        if cancelled {
            lock.unlock()
            return
        }
        if captureEnded {
            // Released before the microphone opened: nothing was heard.
            lock.unlock()
            return bridge.emitSession(Event(generation: generation, kind: VB_EV_FINAL))
        }
        do {
            chunks = try capture.start(to: format) { [weak self] in self?.lost() }
        } catch {
            lock.unlock()
            return fail(VB_ERR_MIC_UNAVAILABLE)
        }
        started = true
        // 60 s from capture start, before the analyzer starts (S9).
        let item = DispatchWorkItem { [weak self] in self?.cancel() }
        failsafe = item
        DispatchQueue.global().asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + .milliseconds(Int(maxMs)), execute: item)
        lock.unlock()
        bridge.emitCapture(generation, on: true)
        do {
            let (g, bridge) = (generation, self.bridge)
            try await work.start(chunks) { text in bridge.emitPartial(g, text) }
            let heard = try await work.finish()
            lock.lock()
            let gone = cancelled
            let stopped = stoppedAt
            lock.unlock()
            if gone { return }
            let latency = stopped.map { UInt64(Probe.ms(since: $0)) } ?? 0
            bridge.emitSession(Event(generation: generation, kind: VB_EV_FINAL, value: UInt32(clamping: latency),
                                     text: heard.text, audioMs: heard.audioMs, voicedMs: heard.voicedMs))
        } catch {
            stopCapture()
            lock.lock()
            let gone = cancelled
            lock.unlock()
            // Never the error's text (spec §6.3).
            if !gone { fail(VB_ERR_SPEECH_UNAVAILABLE) }
        }
    }

    /// Release or toggle: the microphone stops now; the transcript finishes
    /// and this session stays cancellable (S4).
    func stopCapture() {
        lock.lock()
        let wasRunning = started && !captureEnded
        captureEnded = true
        if stoppedAt == nil { stoppedAt = .now }
        failsafe?.cancel()
        lock.unlock()
        capture.stop()
        if wasRunning { bridge.emitCapture(generation, on: false) }
    }

    /// Cancel, final timeout, lock, replacement, failsafe: capture stops now
    /// and all work is cancelled, finishing or not (S4).
    func cancel() {
        lock.lock()
        if cancelled {
            lock.unlock()
            return
        }
        cancelled = true
        let wasRunning = started && !captureEnded
        captureEnded = true
        failsafe?.cancel()
        let t = task
        lock.unlock()
        capture.stop()
        if wasRunning { bridge.emitCapture(generation, on: false) }
        t?.cancel()
        let work = self.work
        Task.detached { await work.cancel() }
    }

    /// The device went away: tell Rust while this is still the live
    /// session, then stop everything.
    private func lost() {
        fail(VB_ERR_MIC_UNAVAILABLE)
        cancel()
    }

    private func fail(_ code: Int32) {
        bridge.emitSession(Event(generation: generation, kind: VB_EV_ERROR, code: UInt32(code)))
    }
}

/// Every session that holds the microphone or still has work (plan S4).
/// Safety commands find their session here, under `lock`, and act on it
/// before the C call returns. `lock` guards `all`.
final class Sessions: @unchecked Sendable {
    static let shared = Sessions(bridge: .shared, make: Sessions.live)

    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var all: [UInt64: Session] = [:]
    private let bridge: Bridge
    private let make: (UInt64) -> Session?
    /// Test hook: called when a session's work ended.
    var onDone: ((UInt64) -> Void)?

    init(bridge: Bridge, make: @escaping (UInt64) -> Session?) {
        self.bridge = bridge
        self.make = make
    }

    var count: Int {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        return all.count
    }

    /// Cancels every other session first; each old capture has stopped
    /// before this returns, so the new one never overlaps it.
    func start(_ generation: UInt64, maxMs: UInt32) -> Int32 {
        guard generation != 0 else { return VB_ERR_FAILED }
        guard let session = make(generation) else { return VB_ERR_SPEECH_UNAVAILABLE }
        lock.lock()
        let old = Array(all.values)
        all = [generation: session]
        lock.unlock()
        old.forEach { $0.cancel() }
        bridge.setCurrent(generation)
        session.run(maxMs: maxMs) { [weak self, weak session] in
            guard let self, let session else { return }
            self.remove(session)
            self.onDone?(session.generation)
        }
        return VB_OK
    }

    func stop(_ generation: UInt64) {
        lock.lock()
        let s = all[generation]
        lock.unlock()
        s?.stopCapture()
    }

    func cancel(_ generation: UInt64) {
        lock.lock()
        let s = all.removeValue(forKey: generation)
        lock.unlock()
        guard let s else { return }
        if bridge.currentGeneration == generation { bridge.setCurrent(0) }
        s.cancel()
    }

    /// Lock or sleep (spec §6.2).
    func cancelAll() {
        lock.lock()
        let ss = Array(all.values)
        all = [:]
        lock.unlock()
        bridge.setCurrent(0)
        ss.forEach { $0.cancel() }
    }

    private func remove(_ session: Session) {
        lock.lock()
        if all[session.generation] === session { all.removeValue(forKey: session.generation) }
        lock.unlock()
    }

    static func live(_ generation: UInt64) -> Session? {
        #if compiler(>=6.2)
        if #available(macOS 26.0, *), let locale = Assets.shared.locale {
            return Session(generation: generation, capture: Capture(), work: Transcription(locale: locale), bridge: .shared)
        }
        #endif
        return nil
    }
}

@_cdecl("vb_session_start")
public func vb_session_start(_ generation: UInt64, _ maxMs: UInt32, _ speechRequired: UInt32) -> Int32 {
    guard Bridge.shared.isInitialized else { return VB_ERR_NOT_INITIALIZED }
    guard generation != 0 else { return VB_ERR_FAILED }
    guard Permissions.status(VB_PERM_MICROPHONE) == VB_PS_GRANTED else { return VB_ERR_MIC_DENIED }
    if speechRequired != 0, Permissions.status(VB_PERM_SPEECH) != VB_PS_GRANTED { return VB_ERR_SPEECH_DENIED }
    switch Assets.shared.caps?.code {
    case .some(VB_UA_NONE): break
    case .some(VB_UA_ASSETS_MISSING): return VB_ERR_ASSETS_MISSING
    default: return VB_ERR_SPEECH_UNAVAILABLE // includes "not checked yet"
    }
    return Sessions.shared.start(generation, maxMs: maxMs)
}

@_cdecl("vb_session_stop")
public func vb_session_stop(_ generation: UInt64) {
    Sessions.shared.stop(generation)
}

@_cdecl("vb_session_cancel")
public func vb_session_cancel(_ generation: UInt64) {
    Sessions.shared.cancel(generation)
}
```

`app/src-tauri/swift/Sources/VoiceBridge/SystemEvents.swift`:

```swift
import AppKit
import VoiceBridgeC

/// Locking the screen or sleep stops capture (spec §6.2). Swift cancels
/// every session itself, so the microphone stops without waiting for Rust,
/// then latches the event for Rust (never dropped, S5). Main actor: the
/// observers are installed and delivered on the main thread.
@MainActor
final class SystemEvents {
    static let shared = SystemEvents()
    private var tokens: [NSObjectProtocol] = []

    func start() {
        guard tokens.isEmpty else { return }
        let report: @Sendable (UInt32) -> Void = { code in
            Sessions.shared.cancelAll()
            Bridge.shared.emitSystem(code)
        }
        let workspace = NSWorkspace.shared.notificationCenter
        tokens.append(workspace.addObserver(forName: NSWorkspace.willSleepNotification, object: nil, queue: .main) { _ in
            report(VB_SYS_WILL_SLEEP)
        })
        tokens.append(workspace.addObserver(forName: NSWorkspace.screensDidSleepNotification, object: nil, queue: .main) { _ in
            report(VB_SYS_SCREENS_SLEEP)
        })
        tokens.append(workspace.addObserver(forName: NSWorkspace.sessionDidResignActiveNotification, object: nil, queue: .main) { _ in
            report(VB_SYS_SCREEN_LOCKED)
        })
        tokens.append(DistributedNotificationCenter.default().addObserver(
            forName: Notification.Name("com.apple.screenIsLocked"), object: nil, queue: .main
        ) { _ in
            report(VB_SYS_SCREEN_LOCKED)
        })
    }
}
```

```rust
    use acd_voice::listen::{Capabilities, ListenError, ListenEvent, Unavailable};
    use acd_voice::text::VoiceText;
    use std::time::Duration;

    fn with_text(kind: u32, generation: u64, text: &[u8]) -> VbEvent {
        VbEvent { kind, generation, text: text.as_ptr(), text_len: text.len(), ..VbEvent::EMPTY }
    }

    #[test]
    fn session_events_keep_their_generation_and_copy_their_text() {
        let partial = unsafe { decode(&with_text(VB_EV_PARTIAL, 4, b"appr")) };
        assert_eq!(partial, Some(BridgeEvent::Listen(ListenEvent::Partial { generation: 4, text: VoiceText::new("appr") })));
        let fin = VbEvent { audio_ms: 900, voiced_ms: 600, value: 120, ..with_text(VB_EV_FINAL, 4, b"approve") };
        assert_eq!(
            unsafe { decode(&fin) },
            Some(BridgeEvent::Listen(ListenEvent::Final {
                generation: 4,
                text: VoiceText::new("approve"),
                audio: Duration::from_millis(900),
                voiced: Duration::from_millis(600),
            }))
        );
        let err = VbEvent { kind: VB_EV_ERROR, generation: 4, code: VB_ERR_MIC_UNAVAILABLE as u32, ..VbEvent::EMPTY };
        assert_eq!(
            unsafe { decode(&err) },
            Some(BridgeEvent::Listen(ListenEvent::Error { generation: 4, error: ListenError::MicrophoneUnavailable }))
        );
        let shown = format!("{:?}", unsafe { decode(&with_text(VB_EV_FINAL, 4, b"SECRET")) });
        assert!(!shown.contains("SECRET"), "{shown}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn capabilities_map_every_reason() {
        let ev = VbEvent { kind: VB_EV_CAPS, code: VB_UA_NONE, value: 1, value2: 16_000, ..with_text(VB_EV_CAPS, 0, b"en_GB") };
        assert_eq!(
            unsafe { decode(&ev) },
            Some(BridgeEvent::Capabilities {
                caps: Capabilities { unavailable: None, streams_partials: true, sample_rate: 16_000 },
                locale: Some("en_GB".into()),
            })
        );
        for (code, why) in [
            (VB_UA_OS_TOO_OLD, Unavailable::OsTooOld),
            (VB_UA_SDK_TOO_OLD, Unavailable::SdkTooOld),
            (VB_UA_TRANSCRIBER_UNSUPPORTED, Unavailable::TranscriberUnsupported),
            (VB_UA_LOCALE_UNSUPPORTED, Unavailable::LocaleUnsupported),
            (VB_UA_ASSETS_MISSING, Unavailable::AssetsMissing),
            (99, Unavailable::TranscriberUnsupported),
        ] {
            assert_eq!(capabilities(code, 0, 0).unavailable, Some(why), "code {code}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_other_kinds_decode() {
        use super::super::permissions::{Permission, PermissionState};
        let e = |kind, code, value| VbEvent { kind, code, value, ..VbEvent::EMPTY };
        assert_eq!(
            unsafe { decode(&e(VB_EV_PERMISSION, VB_PERM_MICROPHONE, VB_PS_DENIED)) },
            Some(BridgeEvent::Permission { which: Permission::Microphone, state: PermissionState::Denied })
        );
        assert_eq!(unsafe { decode(&e(VB_EV_PERMISSION, 77, VB_PS_DENIED)) }, None, "an unknown permission");
        assert_eq!(unsafe { decode(&e(VB_EV_HOLD, 0, 1)) }, Some(BridgeEvent::Hold { down: true }));
        assert_eq!(unsafe { decode(&e(VB_EV_HOLD, 0, 0)) }, Some(BridgeEvent::Hold { down: false }));
        assert_eq!(unsafe { decode(&e(VB_EV_OTHER_KEY, 0, 0)) }, Some(BridgeEvent::OtherKey));
        assert_eq!(unsafe { decode(&e(VB_EV_TAP_DISABLED, 1, 0)) }, Some(BridgeEvent::TapDisabled { reenabled: false }));
        assert_eq!(unsafe { decode(&e(VB_EV_SYSTEM, VB_SYS_SCREEN_LOCKED, 0)) }, Some(BridgeEvent::System));
        assert_eq!(unsafe { decode(&e(VB_EV_ASSETS, 0, 0)) }, Some(BridgeEvent::Assets { ok: true }));
        assert_eq!(unsafe { decode(&e(VB_EV_ASSETS, VB_ERR_FAILED as u32, 0)) }, Some(BridgeEvent::Assets { ok: false }));
    }

    #[test]
    fn start_codes_map_to_listen_errors() {
        assert_eq!(start_result(VB_OK), Ok(()));
        assert_eq!(start_result(VB_ERR_MIC_DENIED), Err(ListenError::MicrophoneDenied));
        assert_eq!(start_result(VB_ERR_SPEECH_DENIED), Err(ListenError::SpeechDenied));
        assert_eq!(start_result(VB_ERR_ASSETS_MISSING), Err(ListenError::AssetsMissing));
        assert_eq!(start_result(VB_ERR_SPEECH_UNAVAILABLE), Err(ListenError::SpeechUnavailable));
        assert_eq!(start_result(VB_ERR_BUSY), Err(ListenError::Failed));
        assert_eq!(start_result(VB_ERR_NOT_INITIALIZED), Err(ListenError::Failed));
    }

    #[test]
    fn capture_and_overflow_decode() {
        let on = VbEvent { kind: VB_EV_CAPTURE, generation: 7, value: 1, ..VbEvent::EMPTY };
        assert_eq!(unsafe { decode(&on) }, Some(BridgeEvent::Capture { generation: 7, on: true }));
        let off = VbEvent { value: 0, ..on };
        assert_eq!(unsafe { decode(&off) }, Some(BridgeEvent::Capture { generation: 7, on: false }));
        assert_eq!(unsafe { decode(&VbEvent { kind: VB_EV_OVERFLOW, ..VbEvent::EMPTY }) }, Some(BridgeEvent::Overflow));
    }

    /// Review Focus 2, spec §4.2, plan S5: the queue is bounded, the
    /// callback never waits, and a lost event reaches the controller as
    /// `Overflow` before the next event.
    #[tokio::test]
    async fn an_overflow_is_reported_before_the_next_event() {
        use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
        let (link, rx) = Link::with_capacity(1);
        assert!(offer(&link, BridgeEvent::OtherKey));
        assert!(!offer(&link, BridgeEvent::Hold { down: false }), "full: dropped and latched");
        let got = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::new()));
        let sink = got.clone();
        let task = tokio::spawn(pump(link.clone(), rx, move |ev| sink.lock().unwrap().push(ev)));
        tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(2), async {
            while got.lock().unwrap().len() < 2 {
                tokio::task::yield_now().await;
            }
        })
        .await
        .expect("the pump delivers");
        task.abort();
        assert_eq!(*got.lock().unwrap(), [BridgeEvent::Overflow, BridgeEvent::OtherKey]);
    }
```

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn codes_round_trip() {
        for p in [Permission::Microphone, Permission::Speech, Permission::InputMonitoring] {
            assert_eq!(Permission::from_code(p.code()), Some(p));
        }
        assert_eq!(Permission::from_code(0), None);
        assert_eq!(PermissionState::from_code(1), PermissionState::Granted);
        assert_eq!(PermissionState::from_code(2), PermissionState::Denied);
        assert_eq!(PermissionState::from_code(3), PermissionState::Restricted);
        assert_eq!(PermissionState::from_code(42), PermissionState::NotDetermined);
    }

    /// Plan S19: only three fixed panes can be opened.
    #[test]
    fn privacy_urls_are_fixed_settings_panes() {
        assert_eq!(Permission::Microphone.privacy_url(), "x-apple.systempreferences:com.apple.preference.security?Privacy_Microphone");
        assert_eq!(Permission::Speech.privacy_url(), "x-apple.systempreferences:com.apple.preference.security?Privacy_SpeechRecognition");
        assert_eq!(Permission::InputMonitoring.privacy_url(), "x-apple.systempreferences:com.apple.preference.security?Privacy_ListenEvent");
    }

    /// Spec §6.4: turning voice on asks only for what voice needs and the
    /// user was never asked about.
    #[test]
    fn turning_voice_on_asks_only_what_is_needed_and_unasked() {
        use PermissionState::*;
        let all_unasked = to_request(true, |_| NotDetermined);
        assert!(all_unasked.contains(&Permission::Microphone));
        assert!(all_unasked.contains(&Permission::InputMonitoring));
        assert_eq!(all_unasked.contains(&Permission::Speech), SPEECH_PERMISSION_REQUIRED);
        assert!(!to_request(false, |_| NotDetermined).contains(&Permission::InputMonitoring), "no hold key, no Input Monitoring");
        assert!(to_request(true, |_| Granted).is_empty());
        assert!(to_request(true, |_| Denied).is_empty(), "a denial is not asked again; Settings links to System Settings");
    }

    #[test]
    fn states_serialize_for_settings() {
        assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(PermissionState::NotDetermined).unwrap(), "not_determined");
        assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(Permission::InputMonitoring).unwrap(), "input_monitoring");
    }
}
```

`app/src-tauri/src/voice/permissions.rs`, above the tests. Set the two constants from the spike record (`docs/research/2026-10-12-voice-step2-spike.md`, "Outcomes for the plan"):

```rust
//! Voice permissions (spec §6.4) and the spike's answers (plan S14, S17).
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

/// Whether SpeechTranscriber needs the Speech Recognition permission.
/// From the step-2 spike; `false` unless the spike showed otherwise (S14).
pub const SPEECH_PERMISSION_REQUIRED: bool = false;

/// Whether the event tap works only after a relaunch once Input Monitoring
/// is granted. From the step-2 spike (S17).
pub const TAP_NEEDS_RELAUNCH: bool = false;

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum Permission {
    Microphone,
    Speech,
    InputMonitoring,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum PermissionState {
    NotDetermined,
    Granted,
    Denied,
    Restricted,
}

impl Permission {
    /// `VB_PERM_*`.
    pub fn code(self) -> u32 {
        match self {
            Permission::Microphone => 1,
            Permission::Speech => 2,
            Permission::InputMonitoring => 3,
        }
    }

    pub fn from_code(code: u32) -> Option<Self> {
        match code {
            1 => Some(Permission::Microphone),
            2 => Some(Permission::Speech),
            3 => Some(Permission::InputMonitoring),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    /// The System Settings pane for this permission (plan S19).
    pub fn privacy_url(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Permission::Microphone => "x-apple.systempreferences:com.apple.preference.security?Privacy_Microphone",
            Permission::Speech => "x-apple.systempreferences:com.apple.preference.security?Privacy_SpeechRecognition",
            Permission::InputMonitoring => "x-apple.systempreferences:com.apple.preference.security?Privacy_ListenEvent",
        }
    }
}

impl PermissionState {
    /// `VB_PS_*`.
    pub fn from_code(code: u32) -> Self {
        match code {
            1 => PermissionState::Granted,
            2 => PermissionState::Denied,
            3 => PermissionState::Restricted,
            _ => PermissionState::NotDetermined,
        }
    }
}

/// The permissions to ask for when voice is turned on (spec §6.4): those
/// voice needs that the user was never asked about. The microphone always,
/// Speech Recognition if the spike said so, Input Monitoring only with a
/// hold key.
pub fn to_request(hold_key_on: bool, state: impl Fn(Permission) -> PermissionState) -> Vec<Permission> {
    let mut needed = vec![Permission::Microphone];
    if SPEECH_PERMISSION_REQUIRED {
        needed.push(Permission::Speech);
    }
    if hold_key_on {
        needed.push(Permission::InputMonitoring);
    }
    needed.into_iter().filter(|p| state(*p) == PermissionState::NotDetermined).collect()
}

#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
pub fn status(p: Permission) -> PermissionState {
    // SAFETY: a plain integer in and out (header contract).
    PermissionState::from_code(unsafe { super::bridge::ffi::vb_permission_status(p.code()) })
}

/// Shows the system prompt if never asked; the answer arrives as
/// `BridgeEvent::Permission`.
#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
pub fn request(p: Permission) {
    // SAFETY: a plain integer; returns at once.
    unsafe { super::bridge::ffi::vb_permission_request(p.code()) }
}
```

```rust
/// What the bridge reported, copied out of the callback. `Debug` never
/// shows a transcript: those are `VoiceText`.
#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum BridgeEvent {
    Listen(ListenEvent),
    Capabilities { caps: Capabilities, locale: Option<String> },
    Permission { which: Permission, state: PermissionState },
    /// The hold key went down or up (Task 7B).
    Hold { down: bool },
    /// Another key went down; which one is never sent (S11).
    OtherKey,
    TapDisabled { reenabled: bool },
    /// The screen locked or the Mac is going to sleep; Swift already
    /// dropped the session.
    System,
    Assets { ok: bool },
    /// Swift's microphone started or stopped (S18).
    Capture { generation: u64, on: bool },
    /// Events were lost (S5); the controller fails closed.
    Overflow,
    /// A spike report: statuses, counts and timings, no transcript.
    Probe(String),
}
```

```rust
        VB_EV_FINAL => Some(BridgeEvent::Listen(ListenEvent::Final {

                locale: (!locale.is_empty()).then_some(locale),
            })
        }
        VB_EV_PERMISSION => Permission::from_code(ev.code)
            .map(|which| BridgeEvent::Permission { which, state: PermissionState::from_code(ev.value) }),
        VB_EV_HOLD => Some(BridgeEvent::Hold { down: ev.value != 0 }),
        VB_EV_OTHER_KEY => Some(BridgeEvent::OtherKey),
        VB_EV_TAP_DISABLED => Some(BridgeEvent::TapDisabled { reenabled: ev.value != 0 }),
        VB_EV_SYSTEM => Some(BridgeEvent::System),
        VB_EV_ASSETS => Some(BridgeEvent::Assets { ok: ev.code == VB_OK as u32 }),
        VB_EV_CAPTURE => Some(BridgeEvent::Capture { generation: g, on: ev.value != 0 }),
        VB_EV_OVERFLOW => Some(BridgeEvent::Overflow),
        VB_EV_PROBE => Some(BridgeEvent::Probe(text())),
        _ => None,
    }
```

```rust
/// A Swift error code as a classified error (spec §6.3).
pub fn listen_error(code: i32) -> ListenError {
    match code {
        VB_ERR_MIC_DENIED => ListenError::MicrophoneDenied,
        VB_ERR_MIC_UNAVAILABLE => ListenError::MicrophoneUnavailable,
        VB_ERR_SPEECH_DENIED => ListenError::SpeechDenied,
        VB_ERR_SPEECH_UNAVAILABLE => ListenError::SpeechUnavailable,
        VB_ERR_ASSETS_MISSING => ListenError::AssetsMissing,
        _ => ListenError::Failed,
    }
}

pub fn start_result(code: i32) -> Result<(), ListenError> {
    if code == VB_OK { Ok(()) } else { Err(listen_error(code)) }
}

pub fn capabilities(code: u32, partials: u32, sample_rate: u32) -> Capabilities {
    let unavailable = match code {
        VB_UA_NONE => None,
        VB_UA_OS_TOO_OLD => Some(Unavailable::OsTooOld),
        VB_UA_SDK_TOO_OLD => Some(Unavailable::SdkTooOld),
        VB_UA_LOCALE_UNSUPPORTED => Some(Unavailable::LocaleUnsupported),
        VB_UA_ASSETS_MISSING => Some(Unavailable::AssetsMissing),
        _ => Some(Unavailable::TranscriberUnsupported),
    };
    Capabilities { unavailable, streams_partials: partials != 0, sample_rate }
}
```

Replace Task 1A's `QUEUE`, `TX`, `DROPPED`, `on_event` and `start` with:

```rust
/// The queue from the callback to the app (plan S5).
pub const QUEUE: usize = 64;

/// The link from the callback to the app (plan S5): a bounded queue plus
/// an overflow latch. The callback never blocks, and a lost event is never
/// silent.
pub struct Link {
    tx: mpsc::Sender<BridgeEvent>,
    overflow: AtomicBool,
    wake: tokio::sync::Notify,
}

impl Link {
    pub fn new() -> (Arc<Link>, mpsc::Receiver<BridgeEvent>) {
        Self::with_capacity(QUEUE)
    }

    pub fn with_capacity(n: usize) -> (Arc<Link>, mpsc::Receiver<BridgeEvent>) {
        let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel(n);
        (Arc::new(Link { tx, overflow: AtomicBool::new(false), wake: tokio::sync::Notify::new() }), rx)
    }
}

static LINK: OnceLock<Arc<Link>> = OnceLock::new();

/// Queues `ev` without waiting. A full queue latches an overflow and wakes
/// the pump (S5).
pub fn offer(link: &Link, ev: BridgeEvent) -> bool {
    if link.tx.try_send(ev).is_ok() {
        return true;
    }
    link.overflow.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);
    link.wake.notify_one();
    false
}

/// Hands events to `f` in order. A latched overflow goes first, before the
/// next queued event: cancelling a little early is the safe side (S5).
pub async fn pump(link: Arc<Link>, mut rx: mpsc::Receiver<BridgeEvent>, mut f: impl FnMut(BridgeEvent)) {
    loop {
        let next = tokio::select! {
            ev = rx.recv() => ev,
            _ = link.wake.notified() => {
                if link.overflow.swap(false, Ordering::SeqCst) {
                    f(BridgeEvent::Overflow);
                }
                continue;
            }
        };
        if link.overflow.swap(false, Ordering::SeqCst) {
            f(BridgeEvent::Overflow);
        }
        match next {
            Some(ev) => f(ev),
            None => return,
        }
    }
}

/// Swift calls this from its event queue. It copies and never blocks.
unsafe extern "C" fn on_event(_ctx: *mut c_void, event: *const VbEvent) {
    // SAFETY: Swift passes a valid event for the duration of this call.
    let Some(ev) = (unsafe { event.as_ref() }) else {
        return;
    };
    // SAFETY: same contract for the text it points to.
    let Some(decoded) = (unsafe { decode(ev) }) else {
        return;
    };
    if let Some(link) = LINK.get() {
        offer(link, decoded);
    }
}

/// Starts the bridge once, on the main thread. `None` if it was started
/// before or failed.
pub fn start() -> Option<(Arc<Link>, mpsc::Receiver<BridgeEvent>)> {
    let (link, rx) = Link::new();
    LINK.set(link.clone()).ok()?;
    // SAFETY: `on_event` is a 'static function and the context is unused (S3).
    let code = unsafe { ffi::vb_init(std::ptr::null_mut(), on_event) };
    if code != VB_OK {
        tracing::error!(code, "the voice bridge did not start");
        return None;
    }
    Some((link, rx))
}
```

```rust
/// `SpeechInput` over the Swift bridge (spec §4.1, plan S2). Every call
/// returns at once; events come back through `start`'s receiver.
pub struct MacSpeech {
    caps: Mutex<(Capabilities, Option<String>)>,
}

impl MacSpeech {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self { caps: Mutex::new((Capabilities::CHECKING, None)) }
    }

    pub fn set_capabilities(&self, caps: Capabilities, locale: Option<String>) {
        *self.caps.lock().unwrap() = (caps, locale);
    }

    /// The resolved speech locale, for Settings.
    pub fn locale(&self) -> Option<String> {
        self.caps.lock().unwrap().1.clone()
    }

    pub fn refresh(&self) {
        // SAFETY: no arguments; returns at once.
        unsafe { ffi::vb_query_capabilities() }
    }

    pub fn install_assets(&self) {
        // SAFETY: no arguments; returns at once.
        unsafe { ffi::vb_install_assets() }
    }
}

impl SpeechInput for MacSpeech {
    fn capabilities(&self) -> Capabilities {
        self.caps.lock().unwrap().0
    }

    fn start(&self, generation: u64, max: Duration) -> Result<(), ListenError> {
        let max_ms = u32::try_from(max.as_millis()).unwrap_or(u32::MAX);
        // SAFETY: plain integers; returns at once (header contract).
        start_result(unsafe { ffi::vb_session_start(generation, max_ms, u32::from(SPEECH_PERMISSION_REQUIRED)) })
    }

    fn stop(&self, generation: u64) {
        // SAFETY: a plain integer; returns at once.
        unsafe { ffi::vb_session_stop(generation) }
    }

    fn cancel(&self, generation: u64) {
        // SAFETY: a plain integer; returns at once.
        unsafe { ffi::vb_session_cancel(generation) }
    }
}

/// Keeps the `Arc` type in one place for `VoiceState`.
pub type Speech = Arc<MacSpeech>;
```

```rust
/// Handles one event from the Swift bridge, on the async runtime.
#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
pub fn dispatch(app: &AppHandle, ev: bridge::BridgeEvent) {
    use bridge::BridgeEvent as E;
    let voice = app.state::<VoiceState>();
    match ev {
        E::Listen(e) => voice.controller.speech_event(e),
        E::Capabilities { caps, locale } => {
            voice.speech.set_capabilities(caps, locale);
            emit_status(app);
        }
        E::Permission { .. } | E::Assets { .. } => emit_status(app),
        E::System => voice.controller.system_stop(),
        E::Capture { generation, on } => voice.controller.capture(generation, on),
        E::Overflow => voice.controller.overflow(),
        // Task 7B wires the hold key.
        E::Hold { .. } | E::OtherKey | E::TapDisabled { .. } => {}
        E::Probe(report) => {
            if let Err(error) = app.emit("voice://probe", report) {
                tracing::error!(%error, "could not emit the probe report");
            }
        }
    }
}

/// Tells Settings to fetch the voice status again (Task 9).
pub fn emit_status(app: &AppHandle) {
    if let Err(error) = app.emit("voice://status", ()) {
        tracing::error!(%error, "could not emit the voice status");
    }
}
```

```swift
import CoreGraphics
import XCTest
import VoiceBridgeC
@testable import VoiceBridge

final class FakePort: TapPort, @unchecked Sendable {
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var on = false
    private var ever = false
    private var gone = false
    var source: CFRunLoopSource? { nil }
    func enable(_ on: Bool) { lock.lock(); self.on = on; if on { ever = true }; lock.unlock() }
    var isEnabled: Bool { lock.lock(); defer { lock.unlock() }; return on }
    var wasEverEnabled: Bool { lock.lock(); defer { lock.unlock() }; return ever }
    var invalidated: Bool { lock.lock(); defer { lock.unlock() }; return gone }
    func invalidate() { lock.lock(); gone = true; on = false; lock.unlock() }
}

/// Hands out ports in order, from whichever tap thread asks.
final class Ports: @unchecked Sendable {
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var queue: [FakePort]
    init(_ ports: [FakePort]) { queue = ports }
    func next() -> FakePort? { lock.lock(); defer { lock.unlock() }; return queue.isEmpty ? nil : queue.removeFirst() }
}

/// Plan S11, S26. Synthetic events and fake ports need no permission.
final class TapTests: XCTestCase {
    private func flags(_ keycode: CGKeyCode, _ raw: UInt64) -> CGEvent {
        let e = CGEvent(keyboardEventSource: nil, virtualKey: keycode, keyDown: true)!
        e.type = .flagsChanged
        e.flags = CGEventFlags(rawValue: raw)
        return e
    }

    func testHoldKeyDownUpAndChordsCarryNoKeyCode() {
        let bridge = Bridge()
        let box = Box()
        bridge.install { box.append($0) }
        let ctx = TapContext(keycode: 61, mask: 0x40, token: 1, bridge: bridge)
        let typed = CGEvent(keyboardEventSource: nil, virtualKey: 14, keyDown: true)! // "e"
        ctx.handle(.keyDown, typed) // not held: nothing
        ctx.handle(.flagsChanged, flags(61, 0x80040)) // Right Option down
        ctx.handle(.flagsChanged, flags(61, 0x80040)) // repeat: nothing
        ctx.handle(.keyDown, typed) // a chord
        ctx.handle(.flagsChanged, flags(56, 0x80042)) // Shift while held: a chord too
        ctx.handle(.flagsChanged, flags(61, 0x0)) // Right Option up
        ctx.handle(.flagsChanged, flags(58, 0x80020)) // Left Option is not the hold key
        bridge.events.sync {}
        XCTAssertEqual(box.all.map(\.kind), [VB_EV_HOLD, VB_EV_OTHER_KEY, VB_EV_OTHER_KEY, VB_EV_HOLD])
        XCTAssertEqual(box.all.map(\.value), [1, 0, 0, 0])
        XCTAssertTrue(box.all.allSatisfy { $0.code == 0 && $0.text == nil }, "no key code or text leaves the tap")
    }

    func testStartWithoutInputMonitoringIsRefused() {
        let tap = HoldTap(bridge: Bridge(), granted: { false }, create: { _ in
            XCTFail("never created")
            return nil
        })
        XCTAssertEqual(tap.start(keycode: 61, mask: 0x40), VB_ERR_INPUT_MONITORING_DENIED)
        XCTAssertFalse(tap.running)
    }

    /// S26: stop returns only after the tap thread exited; by then the port
    /// is disabled and invalidated.
    func testStopWaitsForTheTapThreadToExit() {
        let port = FakePort()
        let tap = HoldTap(bridge: Bridge(), granted: { true }, create: { _ in port })
        XCTAssertEqual(tap.start(keycode: 61, mask: 0x40), VB_OK)
        XCTAssertTrue(port.isEnabled)
        tap.stop()
        XCTAssertTrue(port.invalidated, "the thread finished before stop returned")
        XCTAssertFalse(tap.running)
    }

    /// S26: a start that timed out never leaves a tap running, even if the
    /// tap is created later.
    func testATimedOutStartNeverEnablesALateTap() {
        let gate = DispatchSemaphore(value: 0)
        let port = FakePort()
        let tap = HoldTap(bridge: Bridge(), granted: { true }, readyTimeout: .milliseconds(50), create: { _ in
            gate.wait()
            return port
        })
        XCTAssertEqual(tap.start(keycode: 61, mask: 0x40), VB_ERR_TAP_FAILED)
        gate.signal()
        let deadline = Date().addingTimeInterval(2)
        while !port.invalidated && Date() < deadline { usleep(2_000) }
        XCTAssertTrue(port.invalidated)
        XCTAssertFalse(port.wasEverEnabled)
        XCTAssertFalse(tap.running)
    }

    /// S26: a restart replaces the old tap only after the old thread exited.
    func testARestartReplacesTheOldTapAfterItExited() {
        let (first, second) = (FakePort(), FakePort())
        let ports = Ports([first, second])
        let tap = HoldTap(bridge: Bridge(), granted: { true }, create: { _ in ports.next() })
        XCTAssertEqual(tap.start(keycode: 61, mask: 0x40), VB_OK)
        XCTAssertEqual(tap.start(keycode: 58, mask: 0x20), VB_OK)
        XCTAssertTrue(first.invalidated)
        XCTAssertTrue(second.isEnabled)
        tap.stop()
        XCTAssertTrue(second.invalidated)
    }

    /// A context asked to stop forwards nothing more.
    func testAStoppedContextForwardsNothing() {
        let bridge = Bridge()
        let box = Box()
        bridge.install { box.append($0) }
        let ctx = TapContext(keycode: 61, mask: 0x40, token: 1, bridge: bridge)
        ctx.lock.lock()
        ctx.stopRequested = true
        ctx.lock.unlock()
        ctx.handle(.flagsChanged, flags(61, 0x80040))
        bridge.events.sync {}
        XCTAssertTrue(box.all.isEmpty)
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`app/src-tauri/swift/Sources/VoiceBridge/Tap.swift`:

```swift
import CoreGraphics
import Foundation
import VoiceBridgeC

/// A created event tap, behind a protocol so the lifecycle is tested
/// without Input Monitoring.
protocol TapPort: AnyObject {
    var source: CFRunLoopSource? { get }
    func enable(_ on: Bool)
    var isEnabled: Bool { get }
    func invalidate()
}

final class MachTapPort: TapPort {
    private let port: CFMachPort
    init(_ port: CFMachPort) { self.port = port }
    var source: CFRunLoopSource? { CFMachPortCreateRunLoopSource(nil, port, 0) }
    func enable(_ on: Bool) { CGEvent.tapEnable(tap: port, enable: on) }
    var isEnabled: Bool { CGEvent.tapIsEnabled(tap: port) }
    func invalidate() { CFMachPortInvalidate(port) }
}

/// One tap's state (plan S26). The key and mask never change, so a
/// reconfiguration never changes what a running callback reads. `lock`
/// guards `port`, `loop`, `stopRequested` and `created`. `down` belongs to
/// the tap thread.
final class TapContext: @unchecked Sendable {
    let keycode: Int64
    let mask: UInt64
    let token: UInt64
    let bridge: Bridge
    let ready = DispatchSemaphore(value: 0)
    let exited = DispatchSemaphore(value: 0)
    let lock = NSLock()
    var port: TapPort?
    var loop: CFRunLoop?
    var stopRequested = false
    var created = false
    private var down = false

    init(keycode: UInt16, mask: UInt64, token: UInt64, bridge: Bridge) {
        self.keycode = Int64(keycode)
        self.mask = mask
        self.token = token
        self.bridge = bridge
    }

    var shouldStop: Bool {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        return stopRequested
    }

    /// Forwards only the hold key's down and up, and "another key" while it
    /// is down, with no key code (S11). Never changes an event.
    func handle(_ type: CGEventType, _ event: CGEvent) {
        if shouldStop { return }
        switch type {
        case .tapDisabledByTimeout, .tapDisabledByUserInput:
            lock.lock()
            let p = port
            lock.unlock()
            p?.enable(true)
            let back = p?.isEnabled ?? false
            // A key-up may have been lost; Rust cancels a held recording.
            down = false
            bridge.emit(Event(kind: VB_EV_TAP_DISABLED, code: type == .tapDisabledByTimeout ? 1 : 2, value: back ? 1 : 0))
        case .flagsChanged:
            if event.getIntegerValueField(.keyboardEventKeycode) == keycode {
                let now = event.flags.rawValue & mask != 0
                if now != down {
                    down = now
                    bridge.emit(Event(kind: VB_EV_HOLD, value: now ? 1 : 0))
                }
            } else if down {
                bridge.emit(Event(kind: VB_EV_OTHER_KEY))
            }
        case .keyDown:
            if down { bridge.emit(Event(kind: VB_EV_OTHER_KEY)) }
        default:
            break
        }
    }
}

/// The listen-only hold-key tap (spec §4.1) with a serialized lifecycle
/// (plan S26). Start and stop run one at a time on `lifecycle`. `lock`
/// guards `token` and `current`. Lock order: a context's lock, then this
/// one; never the other way round.
final class HoldTap: @unchecked Sendable {
    static let shared = HoldTap()

    private let bridge: Bridge
    private let granted: () -> Bool
    private let readyTimeout: DispatchTimeInterval
    private let create: (TapContext) -> TapPort?
    private let lifecycle = DispatchQueue(label: "dev.agentcaller.voice.tap.lifecycle")
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var token: UInt64 = 0
    private var current: TapContext?

    init(
        bridge: Bridge = .shared,
        granted: @escaping () -> Bool = { Permissions.status(VB_PERM_INPUT_MONITORING) == VB_PS_GRANTED },
        readyTimeout: DispatchTimeInterval = .seconds(1),
        create: @escaping (TapContext) -> TapPort? = HoldTap.realTap
    ) {
        self.bridge = bridge
        self.granted = granted
        self.readyTimeout = readyTimeout
        self.create = create
    }

    var running: Bool {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        return current != nil
    }

    func start(keycode: UInt16, mask: UInt64) -> Int32 {
        lifecycle.sync { startLocked(keycode, mask) }
    }

    func stop() {
        lifecycle.sync { stopLocked() }
    }

    private func startLocked(_ keycode: UInt16, _ mask: UInt64) -> Int32 {
        stopLocked()
        guard granted() else { return VB_ERR_INPUT_MONITORING_DENIED }
        lock.lock()
        token += 1
        let ctx = TapContext(keycode: keycode, mask: mask, token: token, bridge: bridge)
        lock.unlock()
        let thread = Thread { [self] in self.run(ctx) }
        thread.name = "dev.agentcaller.voice.tap"
        thread.start()
        let timedOut = ctx.ready.wait(timeout: .now() + readyTimeout) == .timedOut
        ctx.lock.lock()
        let ok = ctx.created && !timedOut
        if !ok { ctx.stopRequested = true }
        ctx.lock.unlock()
        guard ok else {
            // A tap created after this sees a stale token and never runs.
            lock.lock()
            token += 1
            lock.unlock()
            return VB_ERR_TAP_FAILED
        }
        lock.lock()
        current = ctx
        lock.unlock()
        return VB_OK
    }

    private func stopLocked() {
        lock.lock()
        let ctx = current
        current = nil
        token += 1
        lock.unlock()
        guard let ctx else { return }
        ctx.lock.lock()
        ctx.stopRequested = true
        let loop = ctx.loop
        ctx.lock.unlock()
        if let loop { CFRunLoopStop(loop) }
        // The thread acknowledges its exit before anything is replaced.
        _ = ctx.exited.wait(timeout: .now() + 2)
    }

    private func isCurrent(_ t: UInt64) -> Bool {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        return token == t
    }

    /// The tap thread.
    private func run(_ ctx: TapContext) {
        defer { ctx.exited.signal() }
        guard let port = create(ctx) else {
            ctx.ready.signal()
            return
        }
        let loop = CFRunLoopGetCurrent()
        ctx.lock.lock()
        let stale = ctx.stopRequested || !isCurrent(ctx.token)
        if !stale {
            ctx.port = port
            ctx.loop = loop
            ctx.created = true
        }
        ctx.lock.unlock()
        if stale {
            port.invalidate()
            ctx.ready.signal()
            return
        }
        if let source = port.source { CFRunLoopAddSource(loop, source, .commonModes) }
        // Keeps the run loop alive between events, so the loop below waits
        // instead of spinning.
        let keepAlive = CFRunLoopTimerCreateWithHandler(nil, CFAbsoluteTimeGetCurrent() + 3600, 3600, 0, 0) { _ in }
        CFRunLoopAddTimer(loop, keepAlive, .commonModes)
        port.enable(true)
        ctx.ready.signal()
        while !ctx.shouldStop {
            _ = CFRunLoopRunInMode(.defaultMode, 0.5, false)
        }
        port.enable(false)
        port.invalidate()
        CFRunLoopRemoveTimer(loop, keepAlive, .commonModes)
    }

    static func realTap(_ ctx: TapContext) -> TapPort? {
        let events = CGEventMask(1 << CGEventType.flagsChanged.rawValue) | CGEventMask(1 << CGEventType.keyDown.rawValue)
        guard let port = CGEvent.tapCreate(tap: .cgSessionEventTap, place: .headInsertEventTap, options: .listenOnly,
                                           eventsOfInterest: events, callback: holdTapCallback,
                                           userInfo: Unmanaged.passUnretained(ctx).toOpaque()) else {
            return nil
        }
        return MachTapPort(port)
    }
}

/// The thread's closure holds `ctx` for as long as the tap can call this.
private func holdTapCallback(proxy: CGEventTapProxy, type: CGEventType, event: CGEvent, info: UnsafeMutableRawPointer?) -> Unmanaged<CGEvent>? {
    if let info {
        Unmanaged<TapContext>.fromOpaque(info).takeUnretainedValue().handle(type, event)
    }
    return Unmanaged.passUnretained(event)
}

@_cdecl("vb_tap_start")
public func vb_tap_start(_ keycode: UInt16, _ mask: UInt64) -> Int32 {
    HoldTap.shared.start(keycode: keycode, mask: mask)
}

@_cdecl("vb_tap_stop")
public func vb_tap_stop() {
    HoldTap.shared.stop()
}
```

```rust
/// The voice toggle's default (plan S12). The inbox hotkey is Control+Alt+Space.
pub const DEFAULT_VOICE_TOGGLE: &str = "Control+Alt+V";

/// The key held to talk (spec §8, plan S12).
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum HoldKey {
    #[default]
    RightOption,
    LeftOption,
    RightCommand,
    RightControl,
    Fn,
    Off,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct VoiceSettings {
    /// Off by default (spec §8).
    #[serde(default)]
    pub enabled: bool,
    /// Show the typed command field in the inbox panel (D13) and the probe (S20).
    #[serde(default)]
    pub debug_input: bool,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub hold_key: HoldKey,
    /// `None` turns the toggle off; a missing field means the default.
    #[serde(default = "default_toggle")]
    pub toggle_shortcut: Option<String>,
}

fn default_toggle() -> Option<String> {
    Some(DEFAULT_VOICE_TOGGLE.to_owned())
}

impl Default for VoiceSettings {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self { enabled: false, debug_input: false, hold_key: HoldKey::default(), toggle_shortcut: default_toggle() }
    }
}
```

Add after `static CURRENT_SHORTCUT`:

```rust
/// The voice toggle (plan S12); registered only while voice is on.
static VOICE_SHORTCUT: Mutex<Option<Shortcut>> = Mutex::new(None);

#[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Fired {
    Panel,
    Voice,
}

/// Which action a pressed shortcut belongs to. Anything but the voice
/// toggle keeps toggling the panel, as before.
pub fn route(fired: &Shortcut, voice: Option<Shortcut>) -> Fired {
    if voice.as_ref() == Some(fired) { Fired::Voice } else { Fired::Panel }
}

pub fn voice_shortcut() -> Option<Shortcut> {
    VOICE_SHORTCUT.lock().ok().and_then(|v| *v)
}

/// Parses a voice toggle and refuses the inbox hotkey.
pub fn check_voice(hotkey: &str, panel: Option<Shortcut>) -> Result<Shortcut, String> {
    let shortcut = parse_shortcut(hotkey)?;
    if Some(shortcut) == panel {
        return Err("That is the inbox hotkey. Choose another voice shortcut.".into());
    }
    Ok(shortcut)
}

/// Whether `hotkey` is the voice toggle; the inbox hotkey must not be.
pub fn conflicts_with_voice(hotkey: &str) -> bool {
    parse_shortcut(hotkey).ok().is_some_and(|s| Some(s) == voice_shortcut())
}

/// Registers the voice toggle, or unregisters it with `None`.
pub fn apply_voice(app: &AppHandle, hotkey: Option<&str>) -> Result<(), String> {
    let next = hotkey.map(|h| check_voice(h, current_shortcut())).transpose()?;
    let shortcuts = app.global_shortcut();
    let mut voice = VOICE_SHORTCUT.lock().map_err(|_| "Voice shortcut lock poisoned".to_string())?;
    if *voice == next {
        return Ok(());
    }
    if let Some(shortcut) = next {
        shortcuts
            .register(shortcut)
            .map_err(|error| format!("Could not register {shortcut}: {error}"))?;
    }
    if let Some(old) = std::mem::replace(&mut *voice, next)
        && let Err(error) = shortcuts.unregister(old)
    {
        tracing::warn!(%error, "could not unregister the previous voice shortcut");
    }
    Ok(())
}
```

```rust
//! The voice triggers (spec §4.1, §6.2): the listen-only hold-key tap and
//! the toggle shortcut. Both run only while voice is on (plan S12).
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};

use tauri::{AppHandle, Manager};

use super::VoiceState;
use super::permissions::{self, Permission, PermissionState, TAP_NEEDS_RELAUNCH};
use super::settings::{HoldKey, VoiceSettings};

static HOLD_ACTIVE: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);

/// macOS virtual key code, and the flag bit that is set while that key is down.
pub fn mac_key(key: HoldKey) -> Option<(u16, u64)> {
    match key {
        HoldKey::RightOption => Some((61, 0x0000_0040)),  // NX_DEVICERALTKEYMASK
        HoldKey::LeftOption => Some((58, 0x0000_0020)),   // NX_DEVICELALTKEYMASK
        HoldKey::RightCommand => Some((54, 0x0000_0010)), // NX_DEVICERCMDKEYMASK
        HoldKey::RightControl => Some((62, 0x0000_2000)), // NX_DEVICERCTLKEYMASK
        HoldKey::Fn => Some((63, 0x0080_0000)),           // NX_SECONDARYFNMASK
        HoldKey::Off => None,
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum TapError {
    InputMonitoringDenied,
    Failed,
}

pub fn tap_result(code: i32) -> Result<(), TapError> {
    match code {
        super::bridge::VB_OK => Ok(()),
        super::bridge::VB_ERR_INPUT_MONITORING_DENIED => Err(TapError::InputMonitoringDenied),
        _ => Err(TapError::Failed),
    }
}

pub fn should_start_hold(enabled: bool, key: HoldKey, granted: bool, active: bool) -> bool {
    enabled && key != HoldKey::Off && granted && !active
}

pub fn hold_active() -> bool {
    HOLD_ACTIVE.load(Ordering::SeqCst)
}

/// The system disabled the tap and it did not come back.
pub fn hold_off() {
    HOLD_ACTIVE.store(false, Ordering::SeqCst);
}

fn start_hold(key: HoldKey) {
    let Some((code, mask)) = mac_key(key) else {
        return;
    };
    // SAFETY: plain integers; returns within a second (Tap.swift).
    let result = tap_result(unsafe { super::bridge::ffi::vb_tap_start(code, mask) });
    if let Err(error) = &result {
        tracing::info!(?error, "the hold key is off");
    }
    HOLD_ACTIVE.store(result.is_ok(), Ordering::SeqCst);
}

fn stop_hold() {
    // SAFETY: no arguments.
    unsafe { super::bridge::ffi::vb_tap_stop() }
    HOLD_ACTIVE.store(false, Ordering::SeqCst);
}

/// Makes both triggers match `settings`. Returns the toggle shortcut's
/// registration error, for Settings. `vb_tap_stop` returns only after the
/// old tap thread exited (Task 7A), so the new tap never overlaps it.
pub fn apply(app: &AppHandle, settings: &VoiceSettings) -> Option<String> {
    // The old tap may hold a key-down whose key-up the new tap never sees:
    // cancel a held recording first (plan S26).
    app.state::<VoiceState>().controller.hold_lost();
    stop_hold();
    let toggle = settings.toggle_shortcut.as_deref().filter(|_| settings.enabled);
    let error = crate::hotkey::apply_voice(app, toggle).err();
    let granted = permissions::status(Permission::InputMonitoring) == PermissionState::Granted;
    if should_start_hold(settings.enabled, settings.hold_key, granted, false) {
        start_hold(settings.hold_key);
    }
    error
}

/// Starts the hold key if it should run and does not, for example right
/// after Input Monitoring was granted (plan S17).
pub fn refresh(app: &AppHandle) {
    if TAP_NEEDS_RELAUNCH {
        return;
    }
    let voice = app.state::<VoiceState>();
    let settings = voice.settings.lock().unwrap().clone();
    let granted = permissions::status(Permission::InputMonitoring) == PermissionState::Granted;
    if should_start_hold(settings.enabled, settings.hold_key, granted, hold_active()) {
        start_hold(settings.hold_key);
    }
}

/// The toggle shortcut fired.
pub fn toggle(app: &AppHandle) {
    let voice = app.state::<VoiceState>();
    voice.controller.toggle(crate::panel::is_visible());
}
```

`app/src-tauri/src/voice/commands.rs`: replace `voice_get_settings` and `voice_set_settings` with:

```rust
#[tauri::command]
pub fn voice_get_settings(voice: State<'_, VoiceState>) -> VoiceSettings {
    voice.settings.lock().unwrap().clone()
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn voice_set_settings(
    app: AppHandle,
    voice: State<'_, VoiceState>,
    settings: VoiceSettings,
) -> Result<VoiceSettings, String> {
    if let Some(toggle) = &settings.toggle_shortcut {
        crate::hotkey::check_voice(toggle, crate::hotkey::current_shortcut())?;
    }
    if let Some(path) = &voice.path {
        settings.save(path).map_err(|_| "Could not save the voice settings".to_owned())?;
    }
    let was_on = std::mem::replace(&mut *voice.settings.lock().unwrap(), settings.clone()).enabled;
    voice.controller.set_enabled(settings.enabled);
    #[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
    {
        use super::permissions;
        use super::settings::HoldKey;
        // Spec §6.4: ask when voice is turned on.
        if settings.enabled && !was_on {
            for p in permissions::to_request(settings.hold_key != HoldKey::Off, permissions::status) {
                permissions::request(p);
            }
        }
        *voice.trigger_error.lock().unwrap() = super::trigger::apply(&app, &settings);
        super::emit_status(&app);
    }
    #[cfg(not(target_os = "macos"))]
    let _ = was_on;
    if let Err(error) = app.emit("voice://settings", &settings) {
        tracing::error!(%error, "could not emit the voice settings");
    }
    Ok(settings)
}
```

```ts
/** Mirrors the app's voice::settings::HoldKey. */
export type HoldKey = "right_option" | "left_option" | "right_command" | "right_control" | "fn" | "off";

/** Mirrors the app's voice::settings::VoiceSettings. */
export type VoiceSettings = {
  enabled: boolean;
  debug_input: boolean;
  hold_key: HoldKey;
  /** null turns the toggle shortcut off. */
  toggle_shortcut: string | null;
};

export const DEFAULT_VOICE_TOGGLE = "Control+Alt+V";

export const VOICE_OFF: VoiceSettings = {
  enabled: false,
  debug_input: false,
  hold_key: "right_option",
  toggle_shortcut: DEFAULT_VOICE_TOGGLE,
};
```

### Task 8: HUD Listening state, Esc, permission errors and the menu bar indicator

**Execution:** `local`.

Depends on Tasks 3, 4 and 6. Listening never takes key (`Hud::wants_key` is false), so the HUD shows it through the existing non-key path in `voice::hud::apply`. No new key path is added, and none needs `panel::focus_webview`. The menu bar dot follows `HudSink::listening`, which only `Controller::capture` calls, so it shows Swift's capture state (S18).

```rust
        assert_eq!(at(&off, corner_x, corner_y), [0, 0, 0, 0]);
        assert_eq!(at(&on, SIZE / 2, SIZE / 2), [245, 166, 35, 255], "the state colour stays");
    }
```

Add imports `use std::sync::Mutex;` and `use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};`, then:

```rust
/// The live-microphone dot (spec §6.2, plan S18).
pub const MIC: [u8; 4] = [255, 59, 48, 255];

/// The last summary, so the microphone dot and the summary are always
/// drawn together and a view update never erases the dot.
static LAST: Mutex<Option<TraySummary>> = Mutex::new(None);
static LISTENING: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);
```

Replace `summary_icon` with:

```rust
pub fn summary_icon(color: TrayColor, listening: bool) -> Vec<u8> {
    let c = rgba(color);
    let (mid, r) = (SIZE as f32 / 2.0, SIZE as f32 * 0.32);
    let (mic_x, mic_y, mic_r) = (SIZE as f32 * 0.82, SIZE as f32 * 0.18, SIZE as f32 * 0.16);
    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity((SIZE * SIZE * 4) as usize);
    for y in 0..SIZE {
        for x in 0..SIZE {
            let (px, py) = (x as f32 + 0.5, y as f32 + 0.5);
            let (dx, dy) = (px - mid, py - mid);
            let (mx, my) = (px - mic_x, py - mic_y);
            if listening && mx * mx + my * my <= mic_r * mic_r {
                out.extend_from_slice(&MIC);
            } else if dx * dx + dy * dy <= r * r {
                out.extend_from_slice(&c);
            } else {
                out.extend_from_slice(&[0, 0, 0, 0]);
            }
        }
    }
    out
}
```

In `build`, use `summary_icon(TrayColor::Grey, false)`.

Replace `update` with:

```rust
pub fn update(app: &AppHandle, summary: &TraySummary) {
    *LAST.lock().unwrap() = Some(summary.clone());
    render(app);
}

/// The microphone went on or off (spec §6.2).
pub fn set_listening(app: &AppHandle, on: bool) {
    LISTENING.store(on, Ordering::SeqCst);
    render(app);
}

fn render(app: &AppHandle) {
    let summary = LAST.lock().unwrap().clone().unwrap_or(TraySummary { color: TrayColor::Grey, needs_you: 0 });
    let listening = LISTENING.load(Ordering::SeqCst);
    let handle = app.clone();
    if let Err(error) = app.run_on_main_thread(move || {
        if let Some(tray) = handle.tray_by_id("main") {
            if let Err(error) = tray.set_icon(Some(Image::new_owned(summary_icon(summary.color, listening), SIZE, SIZE))) {
                tracing::warn!(%error, "could not update tray icon");
            }
            if let Err(error) = tray.set_title(title(summary.needs_you)) {
                tracing::warn!(%error, "could not update tray title");
            }
        }
    }) {
        tracing::error!(%error, "could not dispatch tray update to main thread");
    }
}
```

```rust
/// Opens one of three fixed System Settings panes (plan S19). Settings and
/// HUD windows only.
#[tauri::command]
pub fn voice_open_privacy(window: WebviewWindow, kind: super::permissions::Permission) -> Result<(), String> {
    if window.label() != "settings" && !hud::is_hud(window.label()) {
        tracing::debug!(label = window.label(), "ignored a System Settings request from another window");
        return Ok(());
    }
    std::process::Command::new("/usr/bin/open")
        .arg(kind.privacy_url())
        .status()
        .map(|_| ())
        .map_err(|_| "Could not open System Settings".to_owned())
}
```

```rust
/// Esc in the inbox panel: cancels a recording first (plan S25), else

#[tauri::command]
pub fn escape(state: State<'_, AppState>, voice: State<'_, crate::voice::VoiceState>) -> bool {

}
```

```ts
/** The System Settings pane that fixes a refusal, if one does (plan S19). */
export function privacyPane(r: Refusal): PermissionKind | null {
  switch (r) {
    case "microphone_denied":
      return "microphone";
    case "speech_denied":
      return "speech";
    case "hold_key_off":
      return "input_monitoring";
    default:

  }
}
```

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use acd_voice::listen::Unavailable;

    fn caps(unavailable: Option<Unavailable>) -> Capabilities {
        Capabilities { unavailable, streams_partials: true, sample_rate: 16_000 }
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_status_reports_availability_and_each_permission() {
        let s = build(caps(None), Some("en_GB".into()), |p| match p {
            Permission::Microphone => PermissionState::Granted,
            Permission::Speech => PermissionState::Denied,
            Permission::InputMonitoring => PermissionState::NotDetermined,
        }, false, true, None);
        assert_eq!(s.unavailable, None);
        assert_eq!(s.locale.as_deref(), Some("en_GB"));
        assert_eq!(s.microphone, PermissionState::Granted);
        assert_eq!(s.input_monitoring, PermissionState::NotDetermined);
        assert_eq!(s.speech.is_some(), SPEECH_PERMISSION_REQUIRED, "Speech Recognition shows only if needed (S14)");
        let v = serde_json::to_value(&s).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(v["input_monitoring"], "not_determined");
        let s = build(caps(Some(Unavailable::AssetsMissing)), None, |_| PermissionState::Granted, true, true, Some("Could not register".into()));
        assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(&s).unwrap()["unavailable"], "assets_missing");
        assert_eq!(s.toggle_error.as_deref(), Some("Could not register"));
    }

    /// Plan S17.
    #[test]
    fn a_relaunch_is_asked_for_only_when_it_would_help() {
        let granted = |_| PermissionState::Granted;
        let s = build(caps(None), None, granted, false, true, None);
        assert_eq!(s.relaunch_for_hold_key, TAP_NEEDS_RELAUNCH);
        assert!(!build(caps(None), None, granted, true, true, None).relaunch_for_hold_key, "running already");
        assert!(!build(caps(None), None, granted, false, false, None).relaunch_for_hold_key, "no hold key wanted");
        assert!(!build(caps(None), None, |_| PermissionState::Denied, false, true, None).relaunch_for_hold_key);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the backend**

`app/src-tauri/src/voice/status.rs`, above the tests:

```rust
//! What Settings shows about voice (spec §6.4, §8, §9). No voice content.
use acd_voice::listen::{Capabilities, Unavailable};
use serde::Serialize;

use super::permissions::{Permission, PermissionState, SPEECH_PERMISSION_REQUIRED, TAP_NEEDS_RELAUNCH};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct VoiceStatus {
    pub unavailable: Option<Unavailable>,
    pub streams_partials: bool,
    pub locale: Option<String>,
    pub microphone: PermissionState,
    /// `None` when Speech Recognition is not needed (plan S14).
    pub speech: Option<PermissionState>,
    pub input_monitoring: PermissionState,
    pub hold_key_active: bool,
    /// Input Monitoring is granted but the tap waits for a relaunch (S17).
    pub relaunch_for_hold_key: bool,
    /// Why the voice shortcut could not be registered.
    pub toggle_error: Option<String>,
}

pub fn build(
    caps: Capabilities,
    locale: Option<String>,
    perm: impl Fn(Permission) -> PermissionState,
    hold_key_active: bool,
    hold_key_wanted: bool,
    toggle_error: Option<String>,
) -> VoiceStatus {
    let input_monitoring = perm(Permission::InputMonitoring);
    VoiceStatus {
        unavailable: caps.unavailable,
        streams_partials: caps.streams_partials,
        locale,
        microphone: perm(Permission::Microphone),
        speech: SPEECH_PERMISSION_REQUIRED.then(|| perm(Permission::Speech)),
        input_monitoring,
        hold_key_active,
        relaunch_for_hold_key: TAP_NEEDS_RELAUNCH
            && hold_key_wanted
            && !hold_key_active
            && input_monitoring == PermissionState::Granted,
        toggle_error,
    }
}

#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
pub fn current(app: &tauri::AppHandle) -> VoiceStatus {
    use acd_voice::listen::SpeechInput;
    use tauri::Manager;

    use super::settings::HoldKey;

    let voice = app.state::<super::VoiceState>();
    let settings = voice.settings.lock().unwrap().clone();
    build(
        voice.speech.capabilities(),
        voice.speech.locale(),
        super::permissions::status,
        super::trigger::hold_active(),
        settings.enabled && settings.hold_key != HoldKey::Off,
        voice.trigger_error.lock().unwrap().clone(),
    )
}
```

Add `#[cfg(target_os = "macos")] pub mod status;` to `voice/mod.rs`. The tests run on macOS, where the app builds; the module is only built there.

Append to `app/src-tauri/src/voice/commands.rs`:

```rust
/// Settings' view of voice. Also retries the hold key, so a grant made in
/// System Settings takes effect when Settings regains focus (plan S17).
#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
#[tauri::command]
pub fn voice_status(app: AppHandle) -> super::status::VoiceStatus {
    super::trigger::refresh(&app);
    super::status::current(&app)
}

/// "Ask" next to a permission that was never asked. Settings window only.
#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
#[tauri::command]
pub fn voice_request_permission(window: WebviewWindow, kind: super::permissions::Permission) {
    if window.label() != "settings" {
        tracing::debug!(label = window.label(), "ignored a permission request from another window");
        return;
    }
    super::permissions::request(kind);
}

/// "Download speech model" (plan S21). Settings window only.
#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
#[tauri::command]
pub fn voice_install_assets(window: WebviewWindow, voice: State<'_, VoiceState>) {
    if window.label() != "settings" {
        tracing::debug!(label = window.label(), "ignored an asset request from another window");
        return;
    }
    voice.speech.install_assets();
}
```

Register the three commands in `main.rs`'s `generate_handler!`, after `voice::commands::voice_open_privacy,`.

Append to `app/src/lib/voice.ts`:

```ts
/** Mirrors voice::permissions::PermissionState. */
export type PermissionState = "not_determined" | "granted" | "denied" | "restricted";

/** Mirrors acd_voice::listen::Unavailable. */
export type Unavailable =
  | "checking"
  | "not_mac"
  | "os_too_old"
  | "sdk_too_old"
  | "transcriber_unsupported"
  | "locale_unsupported"
  | "assets_missing";

/** Mirrors voice::status::VoiceStatus. */
export type VoiceStatus = {
  unavailable: Unavailable | null;
  streams_partials: boolean;
  locale: string | null;
  microphone: PermissionState;
  /** null when Speech Recognition is not needed. */
  speech: PermissionState | null;
  input_monitoring: PermissionState;
  hold_key_active: boolean;
  relaunch_for_hold_key: boolean;
  toggle_error: string | null;
};

/** Spec §9: "Not available on this Mac" with the reason. */
export const unavailableText: Record<Unavailable, string> = {
  checking: "Checking speech recognition on this Mac…",
  not_mac: "Voice needs macOS.",
  os_too_old: "Not available on this Mac: voice needs macOS 26 or later.",
  sdk_too_old: "Not available in this build: it was built without the macOS 26 SDK.",
  transcriber_unsupported: "Not available on this Mac: on-device speech recognition is not supported.",
  locale_unsupported: "Not available on this Mac: English speech recognition is not supported.",
  assets_missing: "The English speech model is not installed.",
};

export const HOLD_KEYS: [HoldKey, string][] = [
  ["right_option", "Right Option"],
  ["left_option", "Left Option"],
  ["right_command", "Right Command"],
  ["right_control", "Right Control"],
  ["fn", "Fn"],
  ["off", "Off"],
];
```

`app/src/components/Settings.svelte`:

Script additions:

```ts
  import {
    HOLD_KEYS,
    VOICE_OFF,
    unavailableText,
    type HoldKey,
    type PermissionKind,
    type PermissionState,
    type VoiceSettings,
    type VoiceStatus,
  } from "../lib/voice";

  let status = $state<VoiceStatus>({
    unavailable: "checking",
    streams_partials: false,
    locale: null,
    microphone: "not_determined",
    speech: null,
    input_monitoring: "not_determined",
    hold_key_active: false,
    relaunch_for_hold_key: false,
    toggle_error: null,
  });
  let toggleDraft = $state("");

  const stateText: Record<PermissionState, string> = {
    granted: "Allowed",
    denied: "Denied",
    restricted: "Restricted",
    not_determined: "Not asked yet",
  };

  type Row = { kind: PermissionKind; name: string; state: PermissionState; why: string };
  const rows = $derived<Row[]>([
    {
      kind: "microphone",
      name: "Microphone",
      state: status.microphone,
      why: "Needed to hear you. Agent Caller listens only while you hold the key, or between two presses of the shortcut.",
    },
    ...(status.speech === null
      ? []
      : [{ kind: "speech" as const, name: "Speech Recognition", state: status.speech, why: "Needed by macOS for on-device transcription." }]),
    {
      kind: "input_monitoring",
      name: "Input Monitoring",
      state: status.input_monitoring,
      why: "Needed only for the hold key. The voice shortcut works without it.",
    },
  ]);

  const loadStatus = () =>
    api.getVoiceStatus().then(
      (s) => (status = s),
      () => {},
    );

  const setHoldKey = (select: HTMLSelectElement) =>
    act(async () => {
      try {
        voice = await api.setVoiceSettings({ ...voice, hold_key: select.value as HoldKey });
      } catch (e) {
        select.value = voice.hold_key;
        throw e;
      }
    }, "Could not change the hold key");

  const saveToggle = () =>
    act(async () => {
      voice = await api.setVoiceSettings({ ...voice, toggle_shortcut: toggleDraft.trim() || null });
    }, "Could not save the voice shortcut");
```

```svelte
    {#if status.unavailable}
      <p class="note" role="status">{unavailableText[status.unavailable]}</p>
      {#if status.unavailable === "assets_missing"}
        <button onclick={() => act(() => api.installSpeechAssets(), "Could not start the download")}>Download speech model</button>
      {/if}
    {:else if status.locale}
      <p class="note">Speech: {status.locale}, transcribed on this Mac{status.streams_partials ? ", with live text" : ""}.</p>
    {/if}

    <div class="permissions" role="group" aria-label="Permissions">
      {#each rows as row (row.kind)}
        <div class="permission">
          <span class="name">{row.name}</span>
          <span class="state" data-state={row.state}>{stateText[row.state]}</span>
          {#if row.state === "not_determined"}
            <button aria-label="Ask for {row.name}" onclick={() => act(() => api.requestVoicePermission(row.kind), "Could not ask for the permission")}>Ask</button>
          {:else if row.state !== "granted"}
            <button aria-label="Open System Settings for {row.name}" onclick={() => act(() => api.openVoicePrivacy(row.kind), "Could not open System Settings")}>Open System Settings</button>
          {/if}
          <p class="note">{row.why}</p>
        </div>
      {/each}
    </div>

    <label>
      Hold key
      <select aria-label="Hold key" value={voice.hold_key} disabled={!voice.enabled} onchange={(e) => setHoldKey(e.currentTarget)}>
        {#each HOLD_KEYS as [key, label] (key)}
          <option value={key}>{label}</option>
        {/each}
      </select>
    </label>
    {#if voice.enabled && voice.hold_key !== "off" && !status.hold_key_active}
      <p class="note">
        {status.relaunch_for_hold_key
          ? "Quit and reopen Agent Caller to use the hold key."
          : status.input_monitoring === "granted"
            ? "The hold key is off. Turn voice off and on to try again."
            : "The hold key needs Input Monitoring."}
      </p>
    {/if}

    <label>
      Voice shortcut
      <input aria-label="Voice shortcut" value={toggleDraft} placeholder="Off" disabled={!voice.enabled} oninput={(e) => (toggleDraft = e.currentTarget.value)} />
    </label>
    <button disabled={!voice.enabled} onclick={saveToggle}>Save shortcut</button>
    {#if status.toggle_error}<p class="note">{status.toggle_error}</p>{/if}

    <p class="note">
      Hold the key to talk, or press the shortcut once to start and again to stop. Recording stops after 60 seconds.
      Audio and text stay on this Mac and are never saved. English only; the system microphone is used.
    </p>
```

### Task 10: Live check on the signed bundle

**Execution:** `local+user`. An agent installs the app and records results. The user speaks, holds keys, locks the screen and changes System Settings.

Depends on every other task (1A–9). This is spec §10's manual checklist for the parts step 2 builds.

**Files:**
- Modify: `docs/research/2026-10-12-voice-step2-spike.md` (add a "Live check" section)
- Modify, only if the check shows they need tuning: `crates/acd-voice/src/listen.rs` (`MIN_VOICED`), `app/src-tauri/swift/Sources/VoiceBridge/Audio.swift` (`voicedDBFS`). Each change comes with the test in `listen.rs` updated to the new value, and the measured reason recorded.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the whole step.
- Produces: the live record; any threshold change with its test.
