- [ ] **Step 3: Walk the checklist (user; the agent records each result)**

| # | Do | Expect |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Settings → turn Voice control off, then on (the spike left it on) | Microphone prompt (and Speech Recognition if S14 = true). The Input Monitoring prompt points to System Settings. The permission rows update without reopening Settings. |
| 2 | Grant Input Monitoring in System Settings, then focus Settings again | The hold-key note goes away, or with S17 = true it says to quit and reopen. |
| 3 | With a request selected in the inbox, hold Right Option, say "approve", release | Listening HUD with a microphone mark and live text. Red dot on the menu bar icon while capture runs, gone right after release (Swift's capture state, S18). HUD shows the countdown with the full command. The request is answered. Note the time from release to the countdown (spec §1 aims at about 1 s). |
| 4 | Press the voice shortcut (Control+Option+V), say "deny", press it again | The same flow through the toggle. |
| 5 | Tap Right Option (under 0.3 s); then hold it 2 s without speaking | Nothing is sent. The HUD hides. |
| 6 | In a text field in another app, hold Right Option and type "e" | "é" is typed there, the recording is cancelled, and nothing is sent. Releasing does nothing. |
| 7 | Say "approve" (countdown running), then hold Right Option at once | The countdown is cancelled at the press. Say "cancel" and release: HUD says "Cancelled". |
| 8 | Say "yes always" on a request that has that option, then hold and say "confirm" | The confirm stays visible under the Listening mark with its buttons disabled. "confirm" starts the countdown. |
| 9 | Hold Right Option for over 60 s | Recording stops at 60 s, the HUD says so, the red dot goes away, and nothing is sent. |
| 10 | Start with the toggle, then lock the screen (Control+Command+Q) | After unlock: no recording, no red dot, nothing sent. |
| 10b | Say "yes always" so a confirm waits, then lock the screen | After unlock: the HUD is hidden, Enter and "confirm" do nothing (S24). |
| 10c | Start with the toggle; press Esc with the inbox panel open | The recording is cancelled and the inbox stays open (S25). |
| 10d | Start with the toggle; click the HUD, then press Esc | The recording is cancelled (S25). |
| 11 | Start with the toggle, then close the lid or choose Apple menu > Sleep | Same as 10. |
| 12 | System Settings → turn the microphone off for Agent Caller; press the trigger | HUD: "Microphone access is off for Agent Caller." with Open System Settings, which opens the Microphone pane. |
| 13 | Turn Input Monitoring off | The hold key stops. Settings says it needs Input Monitoring. The toggle still works. |
| 14 | While listening via the toggle, type in another app | Keys go to that app: Listening never takes focus. With the inbox panel open, it stays open while listening. |
| 15 | Terminal > Secure Keyboard Entry on, Terminal frontmost, hold Right Option | Record what happens. The spike's step 8 predicts it. |
| 16 | Whisper "approve"; then speak with a fan or music in the background | Record whether the silence threshold (S10) ignores whispers or passes noise. Tune only with a measured reason. |
| 17 | Rebuild and reinstall with the same identity (Step 2 without `tccutil`), then repeat 3 | No prompts. Grants and the hold key still work. |

| Spec | Where |
|---|---|
| §2.1 macOS 26, SpeechTranscriber, Swift bridge | Tasks 1A, 5A (S16) |
| §3.5 trigger only; §3.6 on-device only; §3.7 no voice content in logs | Tasks 2, 4 (`spoken_text_never_reaches_the_logs`), 5A–5C (no Swift logging, codes only), 6 (`Debug` test) |
| §3.2 no new power; same checks | Task 4 (S15: `a_spoken_approve_counts_down_like_a_typed_one`, `a_withheld_display_acknowledgement_sends_nothing`, `a_revision_bump_after_the_press_drops_the_spoken_step`, `a_spoken_confirm_answers_only_the_current_confirmation`) |
| §4.1 trigger, capture, transcriber seams; capabilities | Task 2 (`SpeechInput`, `Capabilities`), Task 5A (`AudioCapture`, `SpeechWork`), S2 |
| §4.2 bridge contract: ownership, generations, bounded queues, audio thread, main thread, tests | Tasks 1A, 5B, 5C, 6, 7A (S1–S5, S26) |
| §4.3 snapshot freezes when recording starts | Task 4 (S6) |
| §4.4 press during a countdown cancels it; Listening never takes key; Esc and the HUD control cancel | Tasks 3, 4, 8 (S7, S25) |
| §6.2 hold or toggle; 60 s; < 0.3 s or silent; lock and sleep; microphone indicator | Tasks 2, 4, 5C, 7A, 7B, 8 (S9, S18, S24) |
| §6.3 classified errors only; voice text forgotten on lock | Tasks 3, 4 (S24), 5A–5C, 6 |
| §6.4 permissions requested at voice-on, explained in Settings; stable signing; spike | Tasks 1A, 1B, 6, 7B, 9, 10 |
| §8 voice settings: hold key, toggle shortcut, language, microphone | Tasks 7B, 9; language and microphone deferred (S13) |
| §9 not available, permission denied, no microphone, missing assets, tap disabled | Tasks 3, 5B, 5C, 7A, 7B, 8, 9 |
| §10 Swift bridge XCTest; event tap; app vitest; manual checklist | Tasks 5A, 5B, 5C, 7A, 8, 9, 10 |
